中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体正越来越多地用于长时程研究与工程，但评估这些能力仍然昂贵、耗时，且高度依赖人类专业知识。这引出一个自然问题：LLM 智能体自身能否构建高效的基准，从而保留昂贵参考评估的结论？我们通过将自动基准开发形式化为一个元评估问题来研究这一能力：给定一个任务和一个昂贵的参考基准，要求智能体构建一个成本更低的代理基准，以忠实区分候选模型和解决方案。基于这一形式化，我们提出 EvalBuilder，一个用于从三个关键维度评估此类基准生成能力的框架：保真度、效率和鲁棒性。具体而言，我们评估生成的基准是否保持模型排名、能否泛化到留出模型和新收集的响应，并在大幅降低评估成本的同时提供良好校准且具有区分度的分数。我们进一步提供一个迭代环境，使智能体能够在固定时间预算下改进其基准。我们的框架提供了一个系统化的测试平台和基准，用于理解当前 LLM 智能体何时能够开发有用的评估器，以及基准构建本身如何能够日益自动化。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for long-horizon research and engineering, yet evaluating these capabilities remains expensive, time-consuming, and heavily dependent on human expertise. This motivates a natural question: can LLM agents themselves construct efficient benchmarks that preserve the conclusions of costly reference evaluations? We study this capability by formulating automatic benchmark development as a meta-evaluation problem, where an agent is given a task and an expensive reference benchmark and requested to construct a lower-cost proxy benchmark that faithfully distinguishes among candidate models and solutions. Based on this formulation, we introduce EvalBuilder, a framework for evaluating such benchmark-generation capabilities along three key dimensions: fidelity, efficiency, and robustness. Specifically, we assess whether generated benchmarks preserve model rankings, generalize to held-out models and freshly collected responses, and provide well-calibrated and discriminative scores while substantially reducing evaluation cost. We further provide an iterative environment in which agents can refine their benchmarks under a fixed time budget. Our framework provides a systematic testbed and benchmark for understanding when current LLM agents can develop useful evaluators and how benchmark construction itself can be increasingly automated.

</details>


### [55] [Maximising Transparency in Context Compaction Improves Retention of Facts and Commitments for Lightweight Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20116)
*Felix Woolford*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 面向轻量 coding agent 的透明上下文压缩方法，直接改善长会话中的事实与约束保持，与长程 agent 上下文管理高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出确定性压缩方法 transparent-compact，保留用户与助手原文并显式标记丢失信息；在轻量模型上对承诺与事实回忆任务匹配或优于模型摘要和 pi-vcc，尤其减少自信编造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长编码智能体会话会超出上下文窗口，常用模型摘要压缩会改写内容并隐藏丢失信息；现有研究对轻量级、本地部署或高吞吐场景下压缩技术的有效性关注不足。

Method: 针对开源 Pi 编码智能体提出简单确定性压缩方法 transparent-compact，保证上下文中用户与助手的文本不被模型或算法改写，并显式标记被压缩掉的信息；将其与 Pi 默认模型摘要压缩和 pi-vcc 对比，在四个不同性能模型上评测，重点为 Qwen3.8-27B。

Result: 在探测承诺和事实回忆任务上，transparent-compact 与两种基线持平或更优；在避免自信错误（把未回忆内容断言为真实信息）方面尤其有优势；即使在前沿模型上优势仍存在但更小。

Conclusion: transparent-compact 让轻量级模型无需通过推理或搜索绕开压缩造成的信息损失，从而提升信息回忆与应用，且无需按任务或上下文特定方式组织会话历史。

中文摘要：长时间的编码智能体会话会超出模型的上下文窗口，因此智能体会进行压缩：较早的历史被替换为更短的内容，通常是模型编写的摘要。摘要会进行选择和改写，而智能体无法看到丢失了什么。现有工作已经探索了一系列压缩技术，但相对较少关注它们对轻量级模型的有效性，包括那些在本地部署或用于高吞吐量工作负载的模型。在此，我们提出 transparent-compact，一种用于开源 Pi 编码智能体的简单确定性方法，专为此类模型设计，目的是最大化智能体对其自身压缩上下文的视图的透明度。它保证其上下文中用户和助手的文本不会被模型或算法更改，并明确标记压缩在何处剥离了信息。我们将其与 Pi 默认的模型编写摘要压缩以及 pi-vcc 进行比较，后者是一种流行的确定性方法，具有类似的方法，但不逐字保留文本。我们的基准测试在四个性能水平不同的模型上运行，重点强调 Qwen3.8-27B：一种轻量级模型，处于消费级硬件可本地服务的上限。在探测承诺（用户设定的仅存在于对话中的规则）和事实回忆的测试集上，transparent-compact 与两种替代方案持平或表现更好。特别地，它在避免自信的错误方面显示出优势，即把虚假信息断言为真，而不是承认某些内容未被回忆起来。即使使用前沿模型，其优势仍然存在但较小。我们认为，transparent-compact 使轻量级模型无需通过推理或搜索来绕过压缩所丢失的内容，并因此改善了信息的回忆和应用，而不需要对会话历史进行任务或上下文特定的结构化。

Original abstract: Long coding agent sessions outgrow the model's context window, so agents compact: older history is replaced by something shorter, usually a model-written summary. A summary selects and rewords, and the agent cannot see what was lost. Existing work has explored a range of compaction techniques, but comparatively little attention has been paid to their effectiveness with lightweight models, including those deployed locally or used for high-volume workloads. Here, we present transparent-compact, a simple deterministic method for the open-source Pi coding agent, designed specifically for use with such models and with the purpose of maximising the transparency of the agent's view of its own compacted context. It guarantees that user and assistant prose in its context is unchanged by a model or algorithm, and explicitly flags where compaction has stripped information. We compare it with Pi's default model-written summary compaction and with pi-vcc, a popular deterministic method with similar methods but without verbatim retention of prose. Our benchmarks run on four models of varying performance levels, with emphasis on Qwen3.8-27B: a lightweight model at the top end of what consumer hardware can serve locally. On sets probing commitments (rules the user set which exist only in the conversation) and recall of facts, transparent-compact matched or outperformed both alternatives. In particular, it showed an advantage in avoiding confident blunders, i.e. assertion of false information as opposed to recognizing that something was not recalled. Even with a frontier model, its advantage was present but smaller. We argue that transparent-compact spares lightweight models from reasoning or searching their way around what compaction lost, and results in improved recall and application of information as a result, without the need for task or context-specific structuring of the session history.

</details>


### [56] [Drivers of LLM Consensus Formation Under Competing Incentives: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20200)
*Isabel Jones,Matthew Edwards,Janina A. Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究多 agent LLM 在利益冲突下达成共识的机制与网络结构影响，对多 agent 协作设计有实证参考。

TL;DR: 本文基于网络共识博弈评估LLM多智能体共识机制，发现开源模型通常需全局通信才能达成共识，而ChatGPT 5.6-sol在受限或无通信下也能达成共识，但高度聚集网络会普遍降低共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于多智能体环境，其一致同意既可能是有意协调，也可能是意外涌现；理解具有竞争激励的LLM如何达成共识，有助于解释和设计LLM集体。

Method: 引入基于网络共识博弈的评估：智能体网络需就两种颜色之一达成一致以获取收益，但不同颜色收益不同；研究者改变通信权限与网络结构，并测试六个开源模型和一个闭源模型ChatGPT 5.6-sol。

Result: 开源LLM比可比实验中的人类更少达成共识，且仅在消息可全局传播时达成共识；ChatGPT 5.6-sol在仅与邻居通信或无通信能力时也能达成共识，表现为更低固执性、更高从众倾向，并在消息中提供更多邻居状态信息。但高度聚集图会降低共识，与上述特征无关。

Conclusion: 集体结果同时受系统设计和个体模型特征驱动；LLM共识能力不仅取决于模型自身，也取决于通信范围与网络结构。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）正越来越多地部署于多智能体环境中，这些环境会产生一致同意，既包括需要协调的预期交互，也包括非预期的涌现交互。因此，理解具有竞争激励的LLM如何达成共识，对于更好地解释和设计LLM集体至关重要。我们引入了一种基于网络共识博弈的评估方法，以评估驱动这一过程的机制。在该博弈中，一个智能体网络必须就两种颜色之一达成一致才能获得收益，但智能体因所同意的颜色不同而获得不同的收益。我们改变通信权限和网络结构等组成部分，并评估六个开源模型和一个闭源模型（ChatGPT 5.6-sol）。我们发现，与可比实验中的人类相比，开源LLM达成共识的频率更低，并且只有在消息在整个全局网络中传播时才会达成共识。然而，ChatGPT 5.6-sol能够在通信仅限于其邻居以及完全没有通信能力的情况下达成共识。这是通过更低的固执性和更高的从众倾向，以及在消息中提供更多关于其邻居状态的信息来实现的。然而，无论这些特征如何，将智能体置于高度聚集的图中都会降低共识，这表明系统设计和个体模型特征都会驱动集体结果。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments that produce unanimous agreement, both in intended interactions that need coordination and in unintended emergent interactions. Consequently, understanding how LLMs with competing incentives reach consensus is crucial to better interpret and design LLM collectives. We introduce an evaluation, based on the Network Consensus game, to assess the mechanisms driving this process. Here, a network of agents must agree on one of two colours to receive a payoff, but agents are offered different payoffs for the agreed colour. We vary components such as communication access and network structure and assess six open-source models and one closed model (ChatGPT 5.6-sol).
  We find that open-source LLMs reach consensus less often than humans in comparable experiments and only if messages are communicated across the global network. ChatGPT 5.6-sol, however, is able to reach consensus with communication limited to its neighbours and with no communication ability. This is achieved through lower stubbornness and higher majority-following tendencies, and by providing more information about its neighbours' states in messages. However, situating agents in a highly clustered graph reduces consensus, regardless of these traits, demonstrating that both system design and individual model traits drive collective outcomes.

</details>


### [57] [CASI: Credit Assignment via Set Intervention for Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20293)
*Xinyi Cheng,Tianfu Zhang,Jiwen Li,Xuesen Zhang,Shihao Yu,Pengfei Zhu,Renhuan,Heda Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: 针对 agentic 检索中多动作集合奖励的信用分配（Shapley+集合干预），可迁移到 agent 轨迹级信用分配与训练。

TL;DR: CASI 通过快照式集合干预和 Shapley 聚合为多检索动作提供集合内信用，并在赞助搜索实验中提升策略的留出集合价值，生产 A/B 测试中主指标提升 3.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多检索动作只收到一个集合级奖励时，直接广播奖励虽可提供集合级目标的梯度，却无法确定集合内各动作的联盟依赖归因。

Method: CASI 固定可重放快照组件，只生成检索一次，并在共同条件下评估不同子集以构造反事实；再用 Shapley 聚合得到集合内信用，并通过有界零和优势塑形接入策略优化。

Result: m >= 4 时端点通常不足以确定 Shapley 分配，可加价值下额外联盟评估冗余；五动作赞助搜索 1,200 请求回放显示显著联盟依赖。训练时评估器下 CASI 中位余弦相似度最高，敏感性后端下未超过 Singleton；五次训练中 CASI 策略留出拍卖回放集合价值高于 Singleton 和 Broadcast；10% 流量七天生产 A/B 测试中主广告客户价值指标提升 3.51%。

Conclusion: 集合干预与 Shapley 信用分配可有效缓解广播奖励的归因不足，并在实际系统中提升策略表现，但其相对优势会受评估后端敏感性影响。

中文摘要：大语言模型越来越多地联合生成多个检索动作，而下游系统通常只为所得集合提供一个奖励。将该奖励广播可为止于集合级期望目标提供有效的得分函数（score-function）梯度估计器，但并不能确定依赖于联盟的集合内归因。我们提出 CASI（Credit Assignment via Set Intervention for Agentic Retrieval，基于集合干预的智能体检索信用分配），它通过基于快照的集合干预构建缺失的反事实信息：生成并检索一次，保持可重放的快照组件固定，并在共同条件下评估备选子集。Shapley 聚合将这些边际贡献转换为集合内信用，有界零和优势塑形将其纳入策略优化。对于 m >= 4，单元素、留一法和全集端点通常不能确定 Shapley 分配；在可加集合价值下，额外的联盟评估是冗余的。在一个五动作赞助搜索设置中，对 1,200 个请求的穷举拍卖回放揭示了显著的联盟依赖性。在训练时评估器下，CASI 在被评估的信用规则中达到与回放定义的 Shapley 参考的最高中位余弦相似度；在敏感性后端下，CASI 未超过 Singleton。在五次独立训练运行中，CASI 训练的策略在留出拍卖回放集合价值上高于 Singleton 或 Broadcast 训练的策略。在一项覆盖 10% 流量的随机化七天生产 A/B 测试中，CASI 训练的策略在主广告客户价值指标上相对于生产对照观察到 3.51% 的提升。

Original abstract: Large language models increasingly generate multiple retrieval actions jointly, while downstream systems often provide only one reward for the resulting set. Broadcasting this reward provides a valid score-function gradient estimator for the expected set-level objective, but does not determine a coalition-dependent within-set attribution. We introduce CASI (Credit Assignment via Set Intervention for Agentic Retrieval), which constructs the missing counterfactual information through snapshot-grounded set intervention: generate and retrieve once, hold replayable snapshot components fixed, and evaluate alternative subsets under common conditions. Shapley aggregation converts these marginals into within-set credit, which bounded zero-sum advantage shaping incorporates into policy optimization. For m >= 4, singleton, leave-one-out, and full-set endpoints do not generally determine the Shapley allocation; under additive set values, extra coalition evaluations are redundant. In a five-action sponsored-search setting, exhaustive auction replay over 1,200 requests reveals substantial coalition dependence. Under the training-time evaluator, CASI achieves the highest median cosine similarity to a replay-defined Shapley reference among evaluated credit rules; under a sensitivity backend, CASI does not outperform Singleton. Across five independent training runs, CASI-trained policies achieve higher held-out auction-replay set value than Singleton- or Broadcast-trained policies. In a randomized seven-day production A/B test on 10% of traffic, the CASI-trained policy yields an observed 3.51% lift in the primary advertiser-value metric over production control.

</details>


### [58] [Reflect After Failure: Brain-Inspired Self-Evolving Agents for Embodied Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20312)
*Xin Li,Xiaohuan Gu,Yifan Wang,Shaobin Ling,Tianli Shi,Jingao Xu,Zhimeng Yin,Dapeng Wu,Yun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 脑启发自进化 agent，通过元认知反思诊断失败并抽象可复用技能，反思/记忆/自我改进机制可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出脑启发的自进化智能体 HiMeco，通过海马体-元认知反思诊断感知-探索-规划失败，并将交互程序抽象为可复用参数化技能，在 ESI-Bench 上达到最先进性能，并在无进化条件下将 EmbSpatial-Bench 准确率提升 3.94 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身空间任务依赖部分视觉证据进行复杂多步决策，执行失败可能来自错误感知、探索不足或规划不当，而执行反馈难以定位责任组件，导致简单积累交互经验会强化无效策略。

Method: HiMeco 受元认知监控与海马体记忆巩固启发，使用海马体-元认知反思诊断感知-探索-规划（PEP）失败，比较执行尝试以提炼可复用策略和避错指导；将交互程序抽象为参数化技能，并通过证据契约与基于执行的验证决定技能保留和复用；记忆仅在轮次之间更新，模型参数冻结。

Result: HiMeco 在 ESI-Bench 上取得最先进性能，并在没有进化的情况下将 EmbSpatial-Bench 准确率提升 3.94 个百分点。

Conclusion: HiMeco 通过诊断失败、提炼策略与验证技能，缓解了执行反馈无法归因的问题，避免无效策略被强化，从而提升具身空间任务中的自进化效果。

中文摘要：视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在空间推理方面已取得显著进展，而自进化智能体通过从交互中学习，进一步将这些能力扩展到具身环境。然而，当前的自进化在具身空间任务中仍尤其具有挑战性：基于部分视觉证据进行复杂且多步的决策，使得难以确定一次执行为何失败。失败可能源于错误感知、探索不足或规划不当，但仅凭执行反馈很少能揭示究竟哪个组件应负责。因此，单纯积累交互经验可能会强化无效策略。为解决这一问题，我们提出 HiMeco，一种受大脑启发的、面向具身空间智能的自进化智能体，其灵感来自元认知监控与海马体记忆巩固。通过海马体-元认知反思（Hippocampal-Metacognitive Reflection），HiMeco 诊断感知-探索-规划（PEP）中的失败，然后比较执行尝试，以推导出可复用的策略和用于避免重复错误的指导。它还将交互程序抽象为参数化技能，利用证据契约和基于执行的验证来决定其保留与复用。这些策略和技能指导后续交互，记忆仅在轮次之间更新，模型参数保持冻结。HiMeco 在 ESI-Bench 上取得最先进性能，并且在没有进化的情况下将 EmbSpatial-Bench 上的准确率提升 3.94 个百分点。

Original abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have made substantial progress in spatial reasoning, and self-evolving agents further extend these capabilities to embodied environments by learning from interactions. However, current self-evolution remains particularly challenging in embodied spatial tasks, complex and multi-step decisions from partial visual evidence making it difficult to determine why an execution fails. The failure may stem from misperception, insufficient exploration, or incorrect planning, yet execution feedback alone rarely reveals which component is responsible. As a result, simply accumulating interaction experience can reinforce ineffective strategies. To address this, we propose HiMeco, a brain-inspired self-evolving agent for embodied spatial intelligence, inspired by metacognitive monitoring and hippocampal memory consolidation. Through Hippocampal-Metacognitive Reflection, HiMeco diagnoses failures in perception-exploration-planning (PEP), then compares execution attempts to derive reusable strategies and guidance for avoiding recurring errors. It also abstracts interaction programs into parameterized skills, using evidence contracts and execution-based validation to determine their retention and reuse. These strategies and skills guide subsequent interactions, with memory updated only between rounds and model parameters kept frozen. HiMeco achieves state-of-the-art performance on ESI-Bench, and it also improves accuracy on EmbSpatial-Bench by 3.94 percentage points without evolution.

</details>


### [59] [TreeFlex: Agents Navigating Their Own Search Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20334)
*Dongzhuo Li,Fangzhou Cheng,Weiwei Li,Kevin Han,Haixu Ma,Zhongchang Sun,Ruiyi Li,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang,Yuandong Tian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: agent 自主导航搜索树的 reflect/execute 框架并以树感知 GRPO 联合训练，在 SWE-bench 上提升显著，直接相关。

TL;DR: TreeFlex让智能体策略在搜索树中选择恢复、分支、验证或停止，并用树感知GRPO联合训练；在深度研究和SWE-bench Verified上显著优于ReAct、FoldAgent等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct式智能体在单一上下文中累积观测、中间结果和失败尝试，上下文增长会使陈旧与偏离路径信息挤占容量、遮蔽证据，导致聚焦推理困难。因此需要让智能体主动管理搜索状态，而不是依赖固定遍历。

Method: 将每个中间状态连同摘要作为执行框架维护的搜索树节点；同一语言模型分Reflection和Execution两种角色：Reflection全局查看摘要并选定要恢复的节点及指令，Execution恢复路径并用任务工具执行，直到保存新节点或提交方案，再由Reflection批准或退回。策略据此决定加深、分支/重访、验证或停止，无固定遍历顺序和节点分数。使用树感知GRPO联合训练导航、任务动作、检查点保存和提案审查，并以任务级结果奖励加过程奖励优化。

Result: 4B模型小规模RL且所有方法活跃上下文限制相同：在挑战性深度研究基准上，TreeFlex准确率48.8%，高于扁平ReAct的41.5%和FoldAgent的42.8%，Pass@8约高十个百分点。在SWE-bench Verified上，同一9B模型RL训练下，TreeFlex解决54.9%任务，优于FoldAgent的43.3%及其step-wise变体的45.2%。

Conclusion: 通过让策略自主管理树状搜索、状态恢复与提案审查，并结合树感知联合RL，TreeFlex能缓解单一上下文退化问题，在深度研究和软件工程任务上取得更高准确率与解决率。

中文摘要：一种ReAct风格的智能体会在单一上下文中累积观测、中间结果和失败尝试。随着该上下文增长，陈旧且偏离路径的信息会消耗其有限容量并遮蔽相关证据，使聚焦推理日益困难。我们提出TreeFlex，一个由智能体自身策略决定在何处搜索的框架。智能体将每个中间状态连同简短摘要记录为由执行框架（harness）维护的搜索树中的一个节点。该策略是一个单一语言模型，承担两种角色。在Reflection角色中，它通过这些摘要获得树的全局视图，并选择一个节点以恢复，同时给出指令。在Execution角色中，执行框架恢复通向该节点的路径，模型使用常规任务工具执行该指令，直到它保存一个新节点或提出一个解决方案，随后Reflection批准该方案或将其送回另一个节点。通过这些选择，策略决定是加深当前线路、从更早状态分支或重访、验证提案，还是停止，而无需固定遍历顺序或为每个节点设置分数。我们使用树感知的GRPO适配联合训练导航、任务动作、检查点保存和提案审查，并使用由过程奖励增强的任务级结果奖励。在4B模型上进行小规模RL后，在相同活跃上下文限制下，TreeFlex在一个具有挑战性的深度研究基准上达到48.8%准确率，而扁平ReAct为41.5%，FoldAgent为42.8%，其Pass@8比两者高约十个百分点。在SWE-bench Verified上，当三个系统均使用同一9B模型进行RL训练时，TreeFlex解决了54.9%的任务，而FoldAgent为43.3%，其逐步变体为45.2%。

Original abstract: A ReAct-style agent accumulates observations, intermediate results, and failed attempts in a single context. As this context grows, stale and off-path information consumes its limited capacity and obscures relevant evidence, making focused reasoning increasingly difficult. We introduce TreeFlex, a framework in which the agent's own policy decides where to search. The agent records each intermediate state, with a short summary, as a node in a search tree maintained by the harness. The policy is a single language model that acts in two roles. In the Reflection role, it has a global view of the tree through these summaries and picks a node to resume, together with an instruction. In the Execution role, the harness restores the path that led to that node, and the model carries out the instruction with the usual task tools until it saves a new node or proposes a solution, which Reflection then approves or sends back to another node. Through these choices, the policy decides whether to deepen the current line, branch from or revisit an earlier state, verify a proposal, or stop, without a fixed traversal order or a score for each node. We train navigation, task actions, checkpointing, and proposal review jointly with a tree-aware adaptation of GRPO, using task-level outcome rewards augmented by process rewards. After small-scale RL of a 4B model, with the same active-context limit for all methods, TreeFlex reaches 48.8% accuracy on a challenging deep-research benchmark, versus 41.5% for flat ReAct and 42.8% for FoldAgent, and its Pass@8 is about ten points higher than either. On SWE-bench Verified, with all three systems trained with RL from the same 9B model, TreeFlex resolves 54.9% of tasks, versus 43.3% for FoldAgent and 45.2% for its step-wise variant.

</details>


### [60] [Lifted State Policy Optimization: Internalizing Self-Correction through Transition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20350)
*Ren Zhuang,Ben Wang,Shuifa Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Originally titled "Self-Correction as Transition Geometry: Internalizing Reasoning via Lifted State Policy Optimization."

Selection reason: 以状态转移为单位做信用分配并将自我纠错内化到单次生成，对 agent 自我修正与轨迹信用分配有启发。

TL;DR: LSPO 将把有缺陷推理状态转为更优状态的局部、经成本调整的转移作为学习单元，用连续坐标构成提升状态并由能量函数排序，以塑形奖励和内化损失训练单遍策略；在 Qwen3.5-27B 上以 71.8% 聚合准确率匹配测试时反思的 71.9%，且延迟降低 28%、token 减少 25.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化只排序最终答案，轨迹强化学习只最大化聚合回报，二者都未对将 flawed reasoning state 转变为更好状态的局部变化进行 credit 分配。

Method: 提出 Lifted State Policy Optimization（LSPO），将有限的、cost-adjusted transition 作为主要学习单元；为每个文本答案增广一个学习到的连续坐标，形成 lifted state，并用能量函数评估和排序；用 shaped reward 驱动能量下降并扣除 edit 和 step 成本；用 internalization loss 将选定的低能量答案吸收到 direct generator 中，得到单遍策略。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-27B 上，单遍策略在数学、科学、代码和逻辑基准上达到 71.8% 聚合准确率，匹配测试时反思的 71.9%，同时延迟降低 28%、token 减少 25.8%；该模式在 dense 和 mixture-of-experts 骨干网络上均成立；matched controls 证实 transition credit、lifted coordinate 以及 answer internalization 均有贡献；代码在 https://github.com/Venomrko/lspo。

Conclusion: LSPO 以经成本调整的局部状态转移为核心学习单元，使单遍策略在多个基准上达到与测试时反思相当的准确率，并显著降低延迟与 token 消耗；对照实验验证了关键组件的贡献，代码已开源。

中文摘要：偏好优化对最终答案进行排序，而轨迹强化学习最大化聚合回报，但二者都没有对将存在缺陷的推理状态转变为更好状态的局部变化进行信用分配。我们引入提升状态策略优化（Lifted State Policy Optimization, LSPO），它将这种有限的、经成本调整的转移确立为学习的主要单元。每个文本答案都用一个学习到的连续坐标进行增强，形成一个提升状态，由能量函数对其进行评估和排序。一个塑形奖励驱动能量下降，并扣除编辑和步数成本，而一个内化损失将选定的低能量答案吸收到直接生成器中。在 Qwen3.5-27B 上，所得的单遍策略在数学、科学、代码和逻辑基准上达到 71.8% 的聚合准确率，与测试时反思的 71.9% 相匹配，同时延迟降低 28%，token 减少 25.8%。这一模式在稠密和专家混合骨干网络上均成立，匹配的对照实验证实，转移信用和提升坐标各自都有贡献，答案内化也有贡献。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Venomrko/lspo 获取。

Original abstract: Preference optimization ranks final answers and trajectory RL maximizes aggregate returns, yet neither credits the local change that turns a flawed reasoning state into a better one. We introduce Lifted State Policy Optimization (LSPO), which establishes this finite, cost-adjusted transition as the primary unit of learning. Each textual answer is augmented with a learned continuous coordinate, forming a lifted state that an energy function evaluates and orders. A shaped reward drives energy descent net of edit and step costs, and an internalization loss absorbs selected low-energy answers into the direct generator. On Qwen3.5-27B, the resulting single-pass policy reaches 71.8% aggregate accuracy across mathematics, science, code, and logic benchmarks, matching test-time reflection at 71.9% with 28% lower latency and 25.8% fewer tokens. The pattern holds across dense and mixture-of-experts backbones, and matched controls confirm that transition credit and the lifted coordinate each contribute, as does answer internalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/Venomrko/lspo.

</details>


### [61] [From Relevance to Validity: Zero-Shot Tool Selection via Functional and Contextual Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20388)
*Xiaoxuan Dong,Jin Zhang,Zichen Yuan,Yue Zhou,Zihao Rao,Dexu Yu,Tingyong WU,Wenhao Deng,Junchen Fu,Pengwei Sun,Yang Ran,Bo Lan,Yongxin Ni,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向LLM agent零样本工具选择，提出功能与上下文验证框架，对工具调用和验证机制有可迁移启发。

TL;DR: 提出渐进式验证框架，用基于端点的功能兼容性和任务条件上下文证据补充语义相关性，以解决零样本工具选择中的迁移有效性差距，并在跨域/跨数据集迁移中稳定提升能力排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需要零样本选择未见过领域和上下文中的工具；仅靠语义相关性排序无法保证工具功能兼容或在新上下文中适用，存在迁移有效性差距。

Method: 提出渐进式验证框架，在语义相关性之外引入基于端点的功能兼容性验证和以任务为条件的上下文证据验证。

Result: 在跨领域和跨数据集迁移中持续提升能力排序，在功能或上下文模糊时增益更大；消融实验证实功能验证与上下文验证具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架能够弥补语义匹配的不足，通过功能与上下文验证提高零样本工具选择在迁移场景下的有效性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体日益面临来自未见领域和执行上下文的任务，这使得零样本工具选择对于识别能够满足任务需求的工具至关重要。现有的主流方法主要依赖相关性排序，但仅靠语义匹配无法确保工具在功能上兼容或在新的上下文中仍然适用。我们初步识别出一个“迁移有效性差距”，即语义相关的工具在迁移后仍可能无法满足功能或上下文需求。为了解决这一差距，我们提出所提方法，一个渐进式验证框架，它用基于端点的功能兼容性和以任务为条件的上下文证据来补充语义相关性。在跨领域和跨数据集迁移中，所提方法持续提升能力排序，在功能或上下文模糊情况下增益更大。消融结果进一步证实了功能验证和上下文验证的互补贡献。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face tasks from unseen domains and execution contexts, making zero-shot tool selection essential for identifying tools that can satisfy task requirements. Existing dominant methods mainly rely on relevance ranking, but semantic matching alone cannot ensure that a tool is functionally compatible or remains applicable in a new context. We preliminarily identify a \emph{transfer-validity gap}, where semantically relevant tools may still fail to satisfy functional or contextual requirements after transfer. To address this gap, we propose \methodname{}, a progressive validation framework that complements semantic relevance with endpoint-grounded functional compatibility and task-conditioned contextual evidence. Across cross-domain and cross-dataset transfer, \methodname{} consistently improves capability ranking, with larger gains under functional or contextual ambiguity. Ablation results further confirm the complementary contributions of functional and contextual validation.

</details>


### [62] [SwarmRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement through Evolving Scientific Agent Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20431)
*Qianru Meng,Xi Zhang,Konstantinos Papakostas,Congfeng Cao,Wenxiang Hu,Tianyi Lu,Xiao Zhang,Lun Zhou,Zaiqiao Meng,Zhaochun Ren,Xiaohui Yan,Yougang Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多科学agent群体通过反馈变异与互补重组实现递归自我改进，并复用程序组件，对自动代码/研究优化agent有较强借鉴意义。

TL;DR: SwarmRSI 通过反馈引导集群变异与互补集群重组，让科学智能体集群递归自我改进，在多个基准上取得 SOTA，并证明经验可迁移、程序可被生物学家复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体集群虽能整合不同方向的证据与实现，但糟糕的实验结果使研究者难以判断应修改假设还是实现，且整体程序分数会掩盖对其他智能体有用的组件。因此需要一种机制利用反馈和部分结果支持持续自我改进。

Method: 提出 SwarmRSI 框架；设计 Feedback-Guided Swarm Mutation，利用其他智能体的实验反馈精修假设及其实现；提出 Complementary Swarm Recombination，复用跨智能体保留的假设与互补程序组件；通过 Recursive Swarm Evolution 将二者整合为反馈闭环，以新实验证据指导后续研究。

Result: 在 OpenProblems、NatureBench 和 MLE-Bench Lite 上达到 SOTA 性能；进一步评估显示积累的研究经验能提升留出任务表现；生物学家复现其程序并用于后续生物学分析。

Conclusion: SwarmRSI 能有效演化科学智能体集群，实现递归自我改进，并通过反馈引导变异与互补重组利用部分结果，促进可迁移且可复现的科学研究。

中文摘要：科学研究中的递归自我改进（RSI）旨在通过智能体自身的实验经验提升其研究能力。科学智能体集群可以结合来自不同研究方向的证据和实现，以指导其智能体后续实验。然而，较差的实验结果可能导致难以判断应修改假设还是其实现，而整体程序评分可能掩盖对其他智能体有用的组件。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 SwarmRSI，一个用于递归自我改进的科学智能体集群演化框架。我们设计了反馈引导的集群变异（Feedback-Guided Swarm Mutation），利用来自其他智能体的实验反馈来改进假设及其实现。我们还提出互补集群重组（Complementary Swarm Recombination），以复用保留的假设和跨智能体的互补程序组件，使部分结果能够支持新的研究。递归集群演化（Recursive Swarm Evolution）将两种机制整合为一个反馈循环，利用新的实验证据指导后续研究。在 OpenProblems、NatureBench 和 MLE-Bench Lite 上的实验证明了 SwarmRSI 的最先进（SOTA）性能。进一步评估表明，积累的研究经验可以提高在留出任务上的性能。生物学家也复现了其程序，以进行后续生物学分析。

Original abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) in scientific research aims to improve agents' research capabilities through their own experimental experience. Scientific agent swarms can combine evidence and implementations from different research directions to guide their agents' subsequent experiments. However, poor experimental results can leave unclear whether to revise a hypothesis or its implementation, while overall program scores can hide components useful to other agents. To address these challenges, we introduce SwarmRSI, a framework that evolves scientific agent swarms for recursive self-improvement. We devise Feedback-Guided Swarm Mutation to refine hypotheses and their implementations using experimental feedback from other agents. We also propose Complementary Swarm Recombination to reuse retained hypotheses and complementary program components across agents, allowing partial results to support new research. Recursive Swarm Evolution integrates both mechanisms into a feedback loop, using new experimental evidence to guide subsequent research. Experiments on OpenProblems, NatureBench and MLE-Bench Lite demonstrate SwarmRSI's state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance. Further evaluations show that accumulated research experience improves performance on held-out tasks. Biologists also reproduce its programs for follow-up biological analyses.

</details>


### [63] [The Wealth of Agents: Scaling Agentic AI Across Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20518)
*Austin W. Hanjie,Carlos E. Jimenez,Zirui Wang,Karthik Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究多开发者协作构建多agent系统并在GAIA上验证专门化组合收益，对多agent协作与扩展有方法启发。

TL;DR: 该论文研究多开发者智能体系统（MDAs），并在GAIA基准上显示：多个各自准确率不超过35%的专家智能体组合后可达70%，比高预算通用型单开发者系统高12个百分点；理论模型表明MDAs可激励专业化并更高效利用开发者资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统多由单个开发者构建并完全控制，虽然开发简单，但限制了系统规模与能力上限；因此需要探索多开发者协作构建智能体系统是否能突破个体开发者的能力边界。

Method: 构建开发者的实证与理论模型，比较多开发者智能体系统（MDAs）与单开发者智能体系统（SDAs）；在GAIA通用助手基准上，将多个专业化开发者的智能体组合成MDA进行评测。

Result: 在GAIA上，个体智能体准确率均不超过35%的一组专业化开发者，其智能体组合成MDA后达到70%准确率；该MDA比用每位专家数倍开发预算构建的中位通用型SDA高12个百分点；理论模型显示MDAs可激励专业化并促进聚合开发者资源的高效利用。

Conclusion: 研究初步表明，MDAs可作为智能体AI的新扩展轴，使具备不同能力和资源的开发者通过协作共同推进前沿能力。

中文摘要：如今的智能体系统通常由单个开发者设计、构建和部署，并对所有参与智能体拥有完全控制权。尽管这简化了开发，却限制了智能体系统的潜在规模。我们研究多开发者智能体系统（MDAs），它使开发者能够协作构建智能体系统，从而将前沿能力推进到任何单个开发者独自无法达到的水平。我们构建开发者的实证模型和理论模型，以比较MDAs与单开发者智能体系统（SDAs）。在通用助手基准GAIA上，一群专业化开发者其各自智能体的准确率不超过35%，当他们的智能体在MDA中组合时达到70%。该MDA比用相当于每位专家数倍开发预算构建的中位通用型SDA高出12个百分点。我们的理论模型表明，MDA能够激励专业化，并鼓励更有效地利用聚合的开发者资源。综合来看，我们的结果为MDA作为一种新的扩展轴提供了初步依据：它使具备多样能力和资源的开发者能够协作推进智能体AI。

Original abstract: Today's agentic systems are typically designed, built, and deployed by single developers with full control over all participating agents. While this simplifies development, it limits the potential scale of agentic systems. We study multi-developer agentic systems (MDAs) which enable developers to collaboratively build agentic systems to push frontier capability beyond what any individual developer could achieve alone. We construct empirical and theoretical models of developers to compare MDAs with single-developer agentic systems (SDAs). On GAIA, a general assistant benchmark, a population of specialized developers whose individual agents do not exceed 35% accuracy reaches 70% when their agents are combined in an MDA. This MDA performs 12 percentage points above the median general-purpose SDA built with several times each specialist's development budget. Our theoretical models show how MDAs can incentivize specialization and encourage more efficient use of aggregated developer resources. Put together, our results make a preliminary case for MDAs as a new scaling axis by enabling developers with diverse capabilities and resources to advance agentic AI collaboratively.

</details>


### [64] [ActiveEvidence: Decision-Focused Evidence Graphs with Verifiable Acquisition Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20532)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Peng Zhang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: 提出可验证的证据获取与决策证书机制，虽为多模态场景，但对agent在成本约束下的可靠行动选择与验证有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出可决策认证的主动证据获取框架：在免费元数据/付费观测边界、随机向量成本与冻结轨迹下，用CEIG、决策加权包围和间隙导向细化生成ε-决策证书并停止无关交互。在640例上任务分数0.842、定位F1 0.816、证据召回0.873、1.74次接受调用、286.5K视觉token、742ms p95；密集重放动作一致性0.992、证书率0.988，每决策仅评估19.6而非191.4个昂贵交互项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体需决定视觉证据是否值得获取、查询哪个候选或分辨率以及何时停止；在严格免费元数据/付费观测边界、随机向量成本和冻结执行轨迹下，需要决策级认证来保证未解决交互不会改变动作，同时控制昂贵观测成本。

Method: 将问题建模为可决策认证的主动证据获取；构建反事实证据交互图（CEIG）表示一元价值、带符号二阶交互与有向条件伤害，并维护未解决交互的分数包围；用决策加权包围目标收紧可能改变动作身份的边界；采用间隙导向细化，仅评估可能取代当前获取的交互，并在ε-决策证书满足声明容差时停止。证书模型相对，验证固定学习分数下策略一致最优性；语义质量由冻结后标签单独评估。用成对延续rollout学习付费预览下游价值，在线成本过滤与独立的有限策略族轨迹认证分离，获取监控器配独立验证器将证书绑定到冻结轨迹。

Result: 在640例（384/128/128训练/验证/测试）上，控制器达到任务分数0.842、定位F1 0.816、证据召回0.873、1.74次接受调用、286.5K视觉token、742ms p95延迟。已颁发证书密集重放：动作一致性0.992、证书率0.988，每决策评估19.6而非191.4个昂贵交互项。

Conclusion: 该方法在严格免费元数据/付费观测边界下实现高效且可决策认证的主动证据获取；证书为模型相对，能验证策略一致最优并绑定冻结轨迹，但语义质量需通过冻结后标签独立衡量。

中文摘要：多模态智能体必须决定视觉证据是否值得获取、查询哪个候选或分辨率，以及何时进一步交互不再能改变其决策。我们在严格的免费元数据/付费观测边界、随机向量成本和冻结执行轨迹下研究这一问题，并将其视为可决策认证的主动证据获取。反事实证据交互图（CEIG）表示一元证据价值、带符号的二阶交互和有向条件伤害，同时为未解决的交互维护显式的分数包围；决策加权包围目标收紧可能改变动作身份的边界。间隙导向细化仅评估可能取代当前获取的交互，并在 ε-决策证书表明未解决项无法使所选动作超出声明容差时停止。该证书是模型相对的：它在固定的学习分数下验证策略一致的最优性，而语义质量通过冻结后标签单独度量。成对延续 rollout 学习付费预览的下游价值，在线操作成本过滤器与独立的有限策略族轨迹认证分离，并且带有独立实现验证器的获取监控器将每个证书绑定到冻结轨迹。在 640 个案例（划分为 384/128/128 训练、验证和测试案例）上，控制器达到任务分数 0.842、定位 F1 0.816、证据召回率 0.873、1.74 次接受调用、286.5K 视觉 token 和 742ms p95 延迟。已颁发证书的密集重放显示 0.992 动作一致性和 0.988 证书率，同时每个决策评估 19.6 个而非 191.4 个昂贵交互项。

Original abstract: A multimodal agent must decide whether visual evidence is worth acquiring, which candidate or resolution to query, and when further interaction can no longer change its decision. We study this problem under a strict free-metadata/paid-observation boundary, stochastic vector costs, and a frozen execution trace, and treat it as decision-certifiable active evidence acquisition. A Counterfactual Evidence Interaction Graph (CEIG) represents unary evidence value, signed second-order interaction, and directed conditional harm while maintaining explicit score enclosures for unresolved interactions; a decision-weighted enclosure objective tightens the bounds that can change action identity. Gap-directed refinement evaluates only interactions that can displace the incumbent acquisition and stops once an $ε$-decision certificate shows that unresolved terms cannot change the selected action beyond a declared tolerance. The certificate is model-relative: it verifies policy-consistent optimality under the pinned learned score, and semantic quality is measured separately through post-freeze labels. Paired continuation rollouts learn the downstream value of paid previews, an online operational cost filter is separated from independent finite-policy-family trajectory certification, and an acquisition monitor with an independently implemented verifier binds every certificate to the frozen trace. On 640 cases split into 384/128/128 training, validation, and test cases, the controller reaches task score 0.842, grounding F1 0.816, evidence recall 0.873, 1.74 accepted calls, 286.5K visual tokens, and 742ms p95 latency. Dense replay of issued certificates shows 0.992 action agreement and a 0.988 certificate rate while evaluating 19.6 instead of 191.4 expensive interaction terms per decision.

</details>


### [65] [PhySGA: A Physics-Space Grounded Agentic Framework for Generalizable and Interactive 3D Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20560)
*Weichen Lyu,Chong Xia,Mingyu Dong,Long Xu,Shengjun Zhang,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Project Page: https://agenticei.github.io/PhySGA-page/

Selection reason: 提出带评论agent和自演化技能库的物理空间组合框架，其反馈修正与经验复用机制可迁移到一般agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出PhySGA，一个结合智能体探索与物理仿真的3D组合框架，通过装配智能体、评论家智能体、动作路由器和自进化技能库，在Isaac Sim中实现刚性、铰接与可变形物体的交互式装配，并显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D物体组合是数字内容创作、机器人仿真和基础模型物理智能测试的关键任务，但现有基于LLM坐标预测或扩散先验图像对齐的方法依赖静态先验，难以处理精确悬挂、重力驱动沉降和隐藏包容等需要全局规划、细粒度对齐与物理仿真的复杂组合。

Method: 提出PhySGA物理空间接地智能体框架，通过智能体探索与物理仿真实现通用交互式装配。其核心包括协作式前馈-反馈架构：装配智能体负责几何与运动学推理、全局步骤规划和粗调整；评论家智能体基于接触动力学进行状态诊断和闭环细校正。动作路由器将语义规划映射为仿真器可执行物理原语，任务特定自进化技能库将试错反馈蒸馏为可复用经验。

Result: 在Isaac Sim中的大量实验表明，PhySGA在关系保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量方面显著超越现有方法，并提高成功率、减少探索尝试。

Conclusion: PhySGA通过将智能体推理与物理仿真结合，克服了静态先验在复杂3D组合中的局限，为刚性、铰接和可变形物体的通用交互式装配提供了有效方案。

中文摘要：3D物体组合对于数字内容创作和机器人仿真至关重要，同时也是基础模型物理智能的关键测试平台。此前方法要么利用大语言模型直接预测物体坐标，要么利用生成扩散先验合成组合图像以进行视觉对齐，二者都依赖于数字空间中的静态先验。然而，这些方法在复杂组合如精确悬挂、重力驱动沉降和隐藏包容方面始终表现不佳，因为这些任务需要涉及全局规划、细粒度对齐和物理仿真的交互式构建过程。为应对这些挑战，我们提出PhySGA，一种通过智能体探索与物理仿真实现3D组合的物理空间接地智能体框架，能够实现刚性、铰接和可变形物体的通用交互式装配。直接将原始LLM或VLM应用于该任务被证明不足，因为当前模型本质上缺乏接地的物理理解和交互式构建经验。为克服这一限制，我们设计了一种协作式前馈-反馈架构：装配智能体专注于几何与运动学推理，用于全局步骤规划和粗调整；而评论家智能体基于接触动力学进行推理，用于仿真器状态诊断和细粒度闭环校正。此外，动作路由器将语义规划和分析映射为仿真器中可执行的物理原语，而任务特定的自进化技能库将历史试错反馈蒸馏为可复用经验，从而提高成功率并最小化探索尝试。在Isaac Sim中的大量实验表明，PhySGA在关系保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量方面显著超越现有方法。

Original abstract: 3D object composition is pivotal to digital content creation and robot simulation, while also serving as a critical testbed for physical intelligence in foundation models. Previous methods either leverage large language models to directly predict object coordinates, or utilize generative diffusion priors to synthesize composite images for visual alignment, both of which rely on static priors in digital spaces. However, these methods consistently struggle with complex compositions such as precise hanging, gravity-driven settling and hidden containment, as these tasks require interactive construction processes involving global planning, fine-grained alignment, and physical simulation. To address these challenges, we propose PhySGA, a Physics-Space Grounded Agentic Framework for 3D composition via agent exploration and physical simulation, enabling the universal interactive assembly of rigid, articulated, and deformable objects. Directly applying vanilla LLMs or VLMs to this task proves insufficient, as current models inherently lack grounded physical understanding and interactive construction experience. To overcome this limitation, we design a collaborative feedforward-feedback architecture: an Assembly Agent focuses on geometric and kinematic reasoning for global step planning and coarse adjustment, while a Critic Agent reasons over contact dynamics for simulator state diagnosis and fine-grained closed-loop correction. Furthermore, Action Routers map semantic planning and analysis onto executable physical primitives within the simulator, while a task-specific Self-Evolving Skill Library distills historical trial-and-error feedback into reusable experiences, boosting success rates and minimizing exploration attempts. Extensive experiments within Isaac Sim demonstrate that PhySGA significantly surpasses existing methods in relation fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality.

</details>


### [66] [Scaling Discovery Requires Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20563)
*Shangyin Tan,Ben Zhou,Lakshya A Agrawal,Jialin Zhang,Yilin Chen,Qiuyang Mang,Donghyun Lee,Dennis Jacob,Koushik Sen,Alex Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究LLM agent自动研究中通过遗忘搜索状态突破瓶颈，并在启发式编程与内核优化上验证，直接关联自动代码优化与长程agent记忆管理。

TL;DR: LLM智能体自动化研究在积累历史后陷入平台期；定期遗忘搜索状态并从最佳候选重启，可在相同预算下显著提升发现效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展策略（单智能体长迭代、多智能体并行、并行智能体通信）都会因历史累积导致智能体锚定过往尝试，编辑变少变小，额外预算收益递减。

Method: 提出遗忘机制：定期丢弃搜索状态（各智能体会话与候选历史），从当前最佳候选重启；在带通信的智能体团队中实现，并在启发式编程、内核优化、模型训练四任务评估。

Result: 相比并行智能体最多提升15%，相比单智能体最多提升39%。

Conclusion: 对于开放式发现，智能体被允许记住什么与获得多少计算资源同样重要。

中文摘要：基于LLM智能体的自动化研究已在多个领域展现出良好前景。通过迭代改进和带评分的验证器，LLM智能体能够发现新想法。扩展自动化研究有不同的方式：让一个智能体迭代更长时间、并行运行多个智能体，或进一步让并行智能体相互通信并在彼此的候选方案基础上继续构建。然而，我们发现这三种策略最终都会随着智能体积累历史而进入平台期。每个智能体都锚定于自身过去的尝试，导致编辑越来越少、越来越小。超过这一点后，额外的预算只能带来很小的进一步改进。在本文中，我们表明进一步扩展发现的关键在于遗忘。定期丢弃搜索状态，包括每个智能体的会话和候选历史，并从目前找到的最佳候选重新开始，可以解除搜索阻塞，并让相同预算发挥更大作用。我们在一个具有通信的智能体团队中实现了遗忘，并在涵盖启发式编程、内核优化和模型训练的四个任务上评估。结果表明，与并行智能体相比最多提升15%，与单个智能体相比最多提升39%。我们的结果表明，对于开放式发现，允许智能体记住什么，与其获得多少计算资源同样重要。

Original abstract: Automated research with LLM agents has shown promising results in various domains. Through iterative refinement and scored verifiers, LLM agents are capable of discovering new ideas. There are different ways to scale automated research: let one agent iterate for longer, run several agents in parallel, or further let the parallel agents communicate and build on one another's candidates. However, we find that all three strategies eventually plateau as the agents accumulate history. Each agent becomes anchored to its own past attempts, making fewer and smaller edits. Beyond this point, additional budget brings little further improvement. In this paper, we show that the key to scaling discovery further is to forget. Periodically discarding the search state, including every agent's session and the candidate history, and restarting from the best candidate found so far, unblocks the search and pushes the same budget further.
  We implement forgetting in a team of agents with communication and evaluate it on four tasks spanning heuristic programming, kernel optimization and model training. The results show up to 15% improvement compared to parallel agents, and up to 39% compared to a single agent. Our results suggest that, for open-ended discovery, what an agent is allowed to remember matters as much as how much compute it is given.

</details>


### [67] [Co-Evolving Acting and Forecasting for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20597)
*Haofei Yu,Yixing Chen,Yining Zhao,Xuyan Ye,Yuxiang Liu,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Shuang Yang,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出动作与多步预测协同演化的语言agent训练方法，提升长程任务样本效率和性能，对agent训练与规划有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出 CoPE，利用成功同策略轨迹上的多步动作目标，训练策略主干同时行动与预测，从而在不增加推理开销的情况下提升语言智能体 RL 的样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体 RL 受长时程 rollout 成本限制，且仅用终端标量奖励，中间步骤监督未充分利用。现有价值目标与观测目标难以构建好：价值目标信息单一，观测目标冗长噪声大且仅前瞻一步。因此需要第三类多步动作目标。

Method: 在成功的同策略轨迹上，CoPE 训练共享策略主干根据每个历史生成后续动作序列；每个动作也从先前历史预测并受监督，形成时间集成，使行动与预测共同演化；推理时仍逐步行动，无额外模型或开销。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop、τ²-bench、AppWorld 五个基准上，CoPE 总体性能强于 GRPO 及带预测辅助目标的 RL 基线，并在两个真实应用上提升整体性能；ScienceWorld 上样本效率比 ECHO 和 GRPO 分别最高提升 1.9 倍和 2.0 倍；仅用单个策略主干，无需额外模型、单独预训练或推理开销。

Conclusion: CoPE 通过多步动作目标实现行动与预测协同，提升语言智能体 RL 的样本效率与性能，且推理无额外开销，代码已开源。

中文摘要：训练语言智能体受到长时程 rollout 高成本的瓶颈，然而标准的智能体强化学习（RL）仅从终端标量奖励中学习，使得中间步骤中的监督信息大多未被利用。已有工作通过价值目标（估计每步成功概率）或观测目标（拟合环境响应）来利用这种监督。二者都难以很好地构建：(1) 价值目标来自轨迹中每一步的同一个单一标量信号；(2) 观测目标，如网页和终端日志，冗长且噪声大，并且只能前瞻一步。我们提出了 CoPE，它增加了第三类多步动作目标。在成功的同策略轨迹上，CoPE 训练策略主干网络来生成每个历史之后应跟随的动作序列。每个动作在从先前历史进行预测时也受到监督，形成一个时间集成，共享主干网络在训练期间吸收该集成，从而使行动与预测共同演化。策略在推理时仍然一次执行一步，没有额外开销。在五个基准（ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop、$τ^2$-bench 和 AppWorld）上，CoPE (1) 在模拟环境中取得了比 GRPO 和带有预测辅助目标的 RL 基线更强的总体性能，同时在两个真实应用中提升了整体性能；(2) 在 ScienceWorld 上，样本效率分别比 ECHO 和 GRPO 最高提升 1.9 倍和 2.0 倍；(3) 仅用单一策略主干网络实现这些增益，无需额外模型、单独预训练或推理开销。代码库见 https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/CoPE。

Original abstract: Training language agents is bottlenecked by the high cost of long-horizon rollouts, yet standard agentic reinforcement learning (RL) learns only from a terminal scalar reward, leaving the supervision in intermediate steps largely unused. Prior work exploits this supervision through value targets that estimate per-step success probabilities or observation targets that fit environment response. Both are hard to construct well: (1) value targets come from the same single scalar signal for each step in the trajectory, and (2) observation targets, such as web pages and terminal logs, are long and noisy and reach only one step ahead. We propose \name, which adds a third kind of multi-step action targets. On successful on-policy trajectories, \name trains the policy backbone to generate the action sequences that follows each history. Each action is also supervised when forecast from the preceding histories, forming a temporal ensemble that the shared backbone absorbs during training, and this enables acting and forecasting to co-evolve. The policy still acts one step at a time at inference, with no overhead. Across five benchmarks (ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, $τ^2$-bench, and AppWorld), \name (1) achieves stronger aggregate performance than GRPO and RL baselines with predictive auxiliary targets across simulated environments, while improving overall performance on both realistic applications; (2) delivers up to $1.9\times$ and $2.0\times$ higher sample efficiency than ECHO and GRPO, respectively, on ScienceWorld; and (3) achieves these gains with a single policy backbone, without additional models, separate pretraining, or inference overhead. The codebase is available at https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/CoPE.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [A Systematic Study of Deprecated Android API Usage Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19150)
*Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted for publication in ICECCS2026

Selection reason: 系统研究弃用Android API迁移模式，并用模式引导提示提升代码更新正确率，属可迁移的代码修复/迁移方法。

TL;DR: 本文系统研究已弃用Android API及其替代方案，基于GitHub迁移示例提炼六类迁移模式，并用其增强零样本提示以提升API迁移正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有自动化迁移技术受API变化多样性与上下文复杂性限制，效果有限。

Method: 分析1,000个已弃用API和4,336个GitHub迁移示例，归纳六种迁移类别及涉及版本检查、参数适配、对象初始化、数据类型转换和交互机制变化的使用更新模式；将迁移类别与模式加入零样本GUPPY基准提示。

Result: 在156个基准任务上，模式引导提示将正确更新从139提升到147，并在六类中的五类表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提炼的迁移模式可有效增强自动化迁移提示，提升Android API弃用迁移的正确性。

中文摘要：Android应用程序编程接口（API）持续演进，导致现有API被弃用，并要求开发者将其使用迁移到推荐的替代方案。尽管已有研究提出了自动化迁移技术，但其有效性常受限于API变化的多样性和上下文复杂性。本文对已弃用的Android API、其推荐的替代方案以及开发者在真实世界中进行的使用更新进行了系统性研究。基于对从GitHub收集的1,000个已弃用API和4,336个迁移示例的分析，我们识别出六种迁移类别，并推导出涉及Android版本检查、参数适配、对象初始化、数据类型转换以及API交互机制变化的重复性使用更新模式。为了评估其适用性，我们使用迁移类别和相应的使用更新模式来增强零样本GUPPY基准提示。在156个基准任务上，模式引导的提示将正确更新从139提高到147，并在六种类别中的五类上表现更好。

Original abstract: Android Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) continuously evolve, causing existing APIs to become deprecated and requiring developers to migrate their usages to recommended replacements. Although prior studies have proposed automated migration techniques, their effectiveness is often limited by the diversity and contextual complexity of API changes. This paper presents a systematic study of deprecated Android APIs, their recommended replacements, and real-world usage updates performed by developers. Based on an analysis of 1,000 deprecated APIs and 4,336 migration examples collected from GitHub, we identify six migration categories and derive recurring usage-update patterns involving Android-version checks, parameter adaptation, object initialization, data-type conversion, and changes in API interaction mechanisms. To assess their applicability, we augment the zero-shot GUPPY benchmark prompts with the migration category and corresponding usage-update pattern. On 156 benchmark tasks, the pattern-guided prompt improves correct updates from 139 to 147 and performs better in five of the six categories.

</details>


### [69] [DivAlgo: A systematic evaluation of algorithmic diversity for code generation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19157)
*Anubhav Jangra,Smaranda Muresan*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 24 pages, 18 figures

Selection reason: 提出DivAlgo代码生成多样性benchmark与轻量相似度度量，可用于评测代码agent生成多样性。

TL;DR: 论文提出 DivAlgo 基准和轻量级相似度指标，评估24个代码生成模型的算法多样性，发现模型生成代码多样性远低于人类，最佳策略仅达人类约70%，且过度依赖会缩小程序员算法词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型在专业与教育场景广泛使用，但其生成算法多样化代码的能力尚不清楚，需要大规模评估基准与指标。

Method: 提出 DivAlgo 大规模基准数据集，包含编程问题的算法多样化解决方案；引入轻量级相似度度量以支持大规模评估；在24个现代代码生成模型上开展实验，并测试不同多样化生成策略及引导模型按人类策略生成。

Result: 无控制模型生成代码多样性显著低于人类，仅约31%；最佳多样化生成策略仅约70%；可引导模型采用人类策略，但无法复现人类解决方案的多样性。

Conclusion: 代码生成模型在捕捉人类算法多样性方面存在明显局限；过度接触模型生成代码可能缩窄程序员的算法词汇。

中文摘要：代码生成模型正积极应用于专业和教育环境。然而，大型语言模型（LLMs）生成算法多样化代码的能力在很大程度上仍然未知。我们提出了 DivAlgo 基准，一个大规模数据集，由针对编程问题的算法多样化解决方案构成。我们还引入了一种轻量级相似度度量，以支持大规模评估。我们在24个现代代码生成模型上的实验发现，不受控制的模型生成代码的多样性显著低于人类解决方案（仅捕捉到约31%的人类多样性），即使最好的多样化代码生成策略也只能达到约70%的人类多样性。此外，尽管可以引导模型生成基于人类策略的解决方案，但它们无法复现人类解决方案的多样性。这些发现表明，代码生成模型在捕捉人类所展现的算法多样性方面存在局限，并且过度接触模型生成代码可能会缩小程序员的算法词汇量。

Original abstract: Code generation models are actively being used in professional and educational settings. However, the competence of large language models (LLMs) to generate algorithmically diverse code remains largely unknown. We propose the DivAlgo benchmark, a large-scale dataset constituting algorithmically diverse solutions to programming problems. We also introduce a lightweight similarity metric to enable large-scale evaluation. Our experiments across 24 modern code generation models found that uncontrolled model-generated code is significantly less diverse than human solutions (capturing ~31% of human diversity), and even the best diverse code generation strategy could achieve only ~70% of human diversity. Moreover, while it was possible to steer the models to generate solutions based on human strategies, they were unable to reproduce the diversity of the human solutions. These findings indicate the limitations of code generation models in capturing the algorithmic diversity humans exhibit, and that overexposure to model-generated code can narrow programmers' algorithmic vocabulary.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Functional Correctness: Static Code Quality Profiling of LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19167)
*Jue Huang,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted for publication in ICECCS2026

Selection reason: 对LLM生成代码做大规模静态质量画像（可维护性/可靠性），提供超越功能正确性的评测框架，可迁移到agent产出评估。

TL;DR: 本文利用Pylint、Radon和SonarQube对LiveCodeBench中73个模型生成的65,663个Python程序进行静态质量剖析，发现模型间存在正确性无法反映的可维护性与可靠性差异，且正确性常伴随结构性权衡，呼吁超越通过率评估LLM代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估多关注功能正确性/基准通过率，但基准测试不完整，通过测试不代表代码可维护、可靠或结构良好，因此需要从静态质量角度补充评估。

Method: 从LiveCodeBench收集73个模型的65,663个Python程序，使用Pylint、Radon、SonarQube等静态分析工具评估生成代码，重点关注可维护性和可靠性。

Result: 结果显示模型间存在系统性质量差异，正确性无法捕捉这些差异；可维护性是最大变异来源；正确性往往伴随结构性权衡，而非普遍更简单的解法。

Conclusion: 应构建超越通过率的评估框架，综合衡量LLM生成代码的可维护性、可靠性等静态质量属性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）正越来越多地用于代码生成，然而大多数评估仍然关注生成的代码是否通过基准测试套件。仅功能性正确性只能提供有限的质量视角：基准测试往往不完整，通过它们并不能保证代码是可维护的、可靠的或结构良好的。本文对LLM生成的Python代码进行了大规模静态质量剖析，重点关注可维护性和可靠性。我们使用LiveCodeBench中来自73个模型的65,663个程序，借助包括Pylint、Radon和SonarQube在内的成熟静态分析工具来评估生成的代码。我们的结果表明，模型之间存在系统性差异，这些差异仅靠正确性无法捕捉，其中可维护性成为最明显的变异来源。我们进一步发现，正确性往往伴随着结构性权衡，而不是统一更简单的解决方案。总体而言，我们的发现激励了超越通过率来评估LLM生成代码的评估框架。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet most evaluations still focus on whether generated code passes benchmark test suites. Functional correctness alone, however, provides only a limited view of quality: benchmark tests are often incomplete, and passing them does not guarantee that code is maintainable, reliable, or well-structured. This paper {presents a large-scale static quality profiling of} LLM-generated Python code, with a focus on maintainability and reliability. Using 65{,}663 programs from 73 models in LiveCodeBench, we evaluate generated code with established static analysis tools, including Pylint, Radon, and SonarQube. Our results show systematic differences across models that are not captured by correctness alone, with maintainability emerging as the clearest source of variation. We further find that correctness often comes with structural trade-offs rather than uniformly simpler solutions. Overall, our findings motivate evaluation frameworks that assess LLM-generated code beyond pass rates alone.

</details>


### [71] [OS-Bench: Evaluating SWE Agents on Cross-OS Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19368)
*Qirui Jin,Kenan Li,Liao Zhu,Lingching Tung,Yikai Zhang,Yijia Wu,Zijian Jin,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: OS-Bench是面向SWE agent的跨OS代码迁移可执行基准，含双平台回归测试与多脚手架评测，直接可用于agent能力评测。

TL;DR: 提出 OS-Bench：基于执行覆盖率差异识别 Linux/Windows 平台特定代码，构建 140 个仓库级跨 OS 迁移任务；现有最佳配置成功率仅 26.4%（Linux 目标）和 20.4%（Windows 目标），表明跨 OS 迁移仍极具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级 SWE 基准虽覆盖广泛开发任务，但较少关注跨操作系统代码迁移这一常见且技术上独特的挑战：需将已有实现从一种 OS 迁移到另一种，并同时保持两个系统上的行为正确。

Method: OS-Bench 通过在同一代码仓库的 Linux 和 Windows 上收集并比较执行覆盖率来识别迁移机会；验证平台特定区域，将其组织为五种迁移场景，并转化为仓库级任务，配有目标平台测试和源平台回归测试。

Result: 基准包含来自 111 个仓库的 140 个验证任务，覆盖 C、C++、C#、Go 和 Rust，其中 91 个 Windows→Linux 迁移、49 个 Linux→Windows 迁移；用 SWE-agent、Claude Code、OpenHands、Win-agent 评估六个模型，最佳配置在 Linux 目标任务成功率 26.4%，Windows 目标任务成功率 20.4%，且需保持双系统正确性。

Conclusion: 跨 OS 迁移是困难且尚未充分探索的能力；OS-Bench 可作为改进平台感知 SWE 智能体的测试平台。

中文摘要：语言模型（LM）智能体正越来越多地在源自 GitHub 议题与拉取请求的代码仓库级软件工程（SWE）基准上接受评估。尽管这些基准覆盖了广泛的开发任务，但它们对一项常见却在技术上独特的挑战提供的洞见有限：将现有实现从一个操作系统（OS）迁移到另一个操作系统，同时在两个系统上保持行为一致。我们提出 OS-Bench，一个用于 Linux 与 Windows 之间跨 OS 代码迁移的可执行基准。受案例研究启发——这些案例研究表明跨平台功能常常先在一种 OS 上实现，随后才为另一种 OS 添加支持——OS-Bench 通过比较同一代码仓库在两个系统上收集的执行覆盖率来识别迁移机会。随后，平台特定区域会被验证、组织为五种迁移场景，并转换为带有目标平台测试和源平台回归测试的代码仓库级任务。最终基准包含来自 111 个代码仓库的 140 个经验证任务，涵盖 C、C++、C#、Go 和 Rust，其中包括 91 个 Windows 到 Linux 的迁移和 49 个 Linux 到 Windows 的迁移。我们使用 SWE-agent、Claude Code、OpenHands 和 Win-agent 评估了六个模型。最佳配置在 Linux 目标任务上仅达到 26.4% 的成功率，在 Windows 目标任务上仅达到 20.4% 的成功率，同时还要保持两个系统上的正确性。这些结果表明，跨 OS 迁移是一项具有挑战性且探索不足的能力，而 OS-Bench 是改进平台感知 SWE 智能体的测试平台。

Original abstract: Language-model (LM) agents are increasingly evaluated on repository-level software engineering (SWE) benchmarks derived from GitHub issues and pull requests. Although these benchmarks cover a broad range of development tasks, they provide limited insight into a common yet technically distinct challenge: migrating an existing implementation from one operating system (OS) to another while preserving behavior on both. We introduce OS-Bench, an executable benchmark for cross-OS code migration between Linux and Windows. Motivated by case studies showing that cross-platform features are frequently implemented on one OS before support is added for another, OS-Bench identifies migration opportunities by comparing execution coverage collected from the same repository on both systems. Platform-specific regions are then verified, organized into five migration scenarios, and transformed into repository-level tasks with target-platform tests and source-platform regression tests. The resulting benchmark contains 140 validated tasks from 111 repositories across C, C++, C#, Go, and Rust, comprising 91 Windows-to-Linux and 49 Linux-to-Windows migrations. We evaluate six models with SWE-agent, Claude Code, OpenHands, and Win-agent. The best configurations achieve only 26.4% success on Linux-target tasks and 20.4% on Windows-target tasks while preserving correctness across both systems. These results indicate that cross-OS migration is a challenging and underexplored capability, and OS-Bench is a testbed for improving platform-aware SWE agents.

</details>


### [72] [BenchChecker: Assessing the Credibility of Bug-Fixing Benchmarks for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19447)
*Di Wu,Xu He,Shu Wang,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Published in USENIX Security 2026

Journal reference: 35th USENIX Security Symposium (USENIX Security 26), pp. 5077-5096, 2026

Selection reason: BenchChecker检测bug修复基准的数据污染，提供模型输出+仓库历史的可信评测方法，对agent评测可靠性有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 BenchChecker，通过两阶段取证检测 LLM 代码基准中的数据污染，并在多个基准上验证有效，过滤污染样本会显著降低解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动程序修复基准存在数据污染，测试样本出现在训练集中，导致性能被虚高，需要更可信的评估。

Method: BenchChecker 兼容开源和闭源模型，仅使用文本模型输出和公共仓库历史；先通过代码前缀探测进行仓库存在性测试，再通过相似度对齐和测试套件验证进行补丁存在性测试。

Result: 在 The Stack v2 上训练的 StarCoder-15B、StarCoder2-15B、CodeGen2-16B 上，BenchChecker 在六个基准中 AUC 约 0.9；应用于 SWE-bench Verified 和 Multi-SWE-bench 后，过滤污染样本使多数 LLM 中等难度任务解决率相对原始值下降超过 20%。

Conclusion: BenchChecker 能稳健检测基准污染，揭示受污染样本对评估结果的夸大影响，有助于实现更可信的 LLM 自动程序修复评估。

中文摘要：尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在自动程序修复（APR）方面展现出前景，但现有基准存在数据污染，即测试样本出现在训练集中，从而人为地抬高性能。为解决这一问题，我们提出 BenchChecker，一个兼容开源和闭源模型的框架，通过检测此类污染来支持更可信的评估。基于持续存在的补丁很可能保留在用于训练的最新仓库快照中的观察，BenchChecker 采用基于取证的两阶段检测过程，仅使用文本模型输出以及公共仓库历史。第一阶段通过用代码前缀探测模型来推断训练包含情况，从而进行仓库存在性测试；第二阶段通过相似度对齐和测试套件验证来执行补丁存在性测试，以验证补丁的持续存在。使用在 The Stack v2 上训练的 StarCoder-15B、StarCoder2-15B 和 CodeGen2-16B 进行的评估表明，BenchChecker 在六个基准上实现了稳健检测，AUC 分数约为 0.9。此外，将 BenchChecker 应用于 SWE-bench Verified 和 Multi-SWE-bench 后发现，过滤受污染样本会使大多数 LLM 在中等难度任务上报告的解决率相对于原始率降低超过 20%。

Original abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for Automatic Program Repair (APR), existing benchmarks suffer from data contamination, in which test samples appear in training sets, artificially inflating performance. To address this, we propose BenchChecker, a framework compatible with both open-source and closed-source models that supports more credible evaluation by detecting such contamination. Grounded in the observation that persistent patches likely remain in recent repository snapshots used for training, BenchChecker employs a forensics-based two-stage detection process using only textual model outputs together with public repository history. The first stage conducts a repository presence test by probing the model with code prefixes to infer training inclusion; the second stage performs a patch presence test to verify patch persistence via similarity alignment and test-suite verification. Evaluations using StarCoder-15B, StarCoder2-15B, and CodeGen2-16B trained on The Stack v2 demonstrate that BenchChecker achieves robust detection with AUC scores around 0.9 across six benchmarks. Furthermore, applying BenchChecker to SWE-bench Verified and Multi-SWE-bench reveals that filtering contaminated samples reduces the reported resolution rates of most LLMs by more than 20% relative to the original rates on medium-difficulty tasks.

</details>


### [73] [JevCoder: When Does Decision Delegation Pay Off in Code Repair?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19596)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: JevCoder用低成本概率判定器委派修复决策，在SWE-bench上减少21.6%-50.6% token且提升修复率，直接对应代码agent效率优化。

TL;DR: 论文提出低成本概率评判器 Jev 及工作流 JevCoder，将部分修复决策委托给评判器，以降低编码智能体的 token 消耗，并在多个修复模型上提升修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体修复代码时反复决策，这些决策影响修复轨迹和后续模型请求的 token 成本；需要确定哪些修复决策可委托给低成本概率评判器，以在保持修复质量的同时减少 token 使用。

Method: 让评判器以预定义答案上的概率作答且不生成解释；评估不同修复阶段的判断质量，并测试单个干预对后续修复工作的影响；与无引导修复以及使用修复模型自身作为评判器的同一工作流进行比较；基于这些研究提出 JevCoder，结合代码片段推荐与动作类型引导，并用判断决定何时提交。

Result: 在相同 400 个任务上评估三个修复模型，其中 200 个来自 SWE-bench Verified，200 个来自 SWE-Bench Pro Verified；与无引导修复相比，JevCoder 将修复模型的平均 token 使用量降低 21.6%-50.6%，且三个模型均观察到更高的修复率。

Conclusion: 收益取决于干预所改变的工作，而非判断本身的成本；将部分修复决策委托给低成本概率评判器，可在降低 token 使用的同时维持或改善修复质量。

中文摘要：编码智能体在修复代码时会反复做出决策。这些决策会塑造修复轨迹，并影响后续模型请求的 token 成本。我们研究哪些修复决策可以委托给一个低成本的概率评判器，以在保持修复质量的同时减少 token 使用量。评判器针对问题在预定义答案上给出概率，而不生成解释。我们评估修复各阶段上的判断质量，并测试单个干预如何改变后续修复工作。我们将这些干预与不使用评判器的无引导修复，以及使用修复模型本身作为评判器的同一工作流进行比较。收益取决于干预所改变的工作，而不是仅取决于判断本身的成本。基于这些研究，我们提出 JevCoder，这是一种围绕低成本概率评判器 Jev 构建的工作流。它将代码片段推荐与动作类型引导相结合，并利用判断来决定何时提交。我们在相同的 400 个任务上评估三个修复模型：200 个来自 SWE-bench Verified，200 个来自 SWE-Bench Pro Verified。与无引导修复相比，JevCoder 将修复模型的平均 token 使用量减少了 21.6%-50.6%，并且在三个模型上均观察到更高的修复率。

Original abstract: A coding agent repeatedly makes decisions as it repairs code. These decisions shape repair trajectories and affect the token cost of successive model requests. We ask which repair decisions can be delegated to a low-cost probabilistic judge to reduce token use while maintaining repair quality. The judges answer questions with probabilities over predefined answers, without generating explanations. We evaluate judgment quality across repair stages and test how individual interventions change subsequent repair work. We compare these interventions with unguided repair, which uses no judge, and with the same workflow using the repair model itself as the judge. The benefit depends on the work an intervention changes, rather than the cost of the judgment alone. Based on these studies, we propose JevCoder, a workflow built around Jev, a low-cost probabilistic judge. It combines code-snippet recommendations with action-type guidance and uses judgments to determine when to submit. We evaluate three repair models, each on the same 400 tasks: 200 from SWE-bench Verified and 200 from SWE-Bench Pro Verified. Compared to unguided repair, JevCoder reduces mean repair-model token use by 21.6%-50.6%, with higher observed repair rates across all three models.

</details>


### [74] [When Does the Dependency Graph Help Code-to-Architecture Mapping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19769)
*Rakhshanda Jabeen,Morgan Ericsson,Jonas Nordqvist,Anna Wingkvist*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 将代码到架构映射建模为依赖图上半监督节点分类，属程序分析与图学习方法，可迁移到代码结构理解。

TL;DR: 该文将代码到架构映射建模为依赖图上的半监督节点分类，比较 GCN、GAT、MLP 与朴素贝叶斯；在十个开源 Java 和 C/C++ 系统上，GAT 总体最佳，GCN/GAT 在大型系统 Chrome 上显著优于 MLP，多关系聚合有效，但部分排名受训练随机种子影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构技术债务源于实现偏离预期模块化分解，而源文件到架构模块的映射缺失、不完整或人工维护成本高昂，因此需要自动化且可扩展的代码到架构映射方法。

Method: 构建有向多关系依赖图，以文件为节点、类型化依赖为边，并结合代码标识符与目录布局的词汇特征；采用半监督节点分类与小样本迭代自训练；比较使用相同特征和训练过程的 GCN、GAT、无图 MLP 与朴素贝叶斯参考方法；在十个开源 Java 和 C/C++ 系统上运行 100 次，并后续考察训练随机种子对排名的影响。

Result: 三个神经模型在两个 F1 指标上均优于朴素贝叶斯参考；GAT 的系统级宏平均和微平均 F1 中位数平均值最高，分别为 83.5 和 92.2，MLP 在若干系统上仍有竞争力或更优；在 18,343 个文件的 Chrome 上，GAT 和 GCN 分别比 MLP 高 22 和 19 个宏平均 F1 点，且持续误分类文件显著更少；多关系聚合在所有系统上均优于单一合并边类型；若干模型排名依赖训练随机种子。

Conclusion: 神经消息传递在连通性和同质性有利的场景更有效，GCN 和 GAT 的多关系聚合具有优势；训练随机种子会影响部分较小优势的排名，但 Chrome 上观察到的大图优势依然稳健。

中文摘要：当系统的实现偏离其预期的模块化分解时，就会产生架构技术债务，而源文件与架构模块之间的映射缺失、不完整，或人工维护成本高昂。我们将代码到架构的映射形式化为有向多关系依赖图上的半监督节点分类问题，其中文件为节点，带类型的依赖关系为边，词汇特征则源自代码标识符和目录布局。从一个小规模标注样本开始，迭代式自训练过程将映射扩展到其余文件。我们比较图卷积网络（GCN）、图注意力网络（GAT）和无图多层感知机（MLP），对学习模型使用相同的特征和训练过程，并与朴素贝叶斯参考方法进行对比。在十个开源 Java 和 C/C++ 系统上进行 100 次运行，三个神经模型在两个 F1 指标上均优于参考方法。GAT 在系统级宏平均和微平均 F1 的中位数平均值上最高，分别为 83.5 和 92.2，而 MLP 在若干系统上仍具有竞争力或更优。在最大的系统 Chrome（18,343 个文件）上，GAT 和 GCN 分别比 MLP 高出 22 和 19 个宏平均 F1 点，并且持续误分类的文件显著更少。连通性和同质性与神经消息传递在何处有帮助相关，而对于 GCN 和 GAT，多关系聚合在每个系统上都优于单一合并边类型。一项后续研究发现，若干模型排名取决于训练随机种子，这限定了较小优势，但并未消除在 Chrome 上观察到的大图优势。

Original abstract: Architectural technical debt arises when a system's implementation drifts from its inten ded modular decomposition, while the mapping between source files and architectural modu les is missing, incomplete, or costly to maintain manually. We formulate code-to-archite cture mapping as semi-supervised node classification over a directed multi-relational de pendency graph, with files as nodes, typed dependencies as edges, and lexical features d erived from code identifiers and directory layout. Starting from a small labeled sample,
  an iterative self-training procedure expands the mapping to the remaining files. We com pare a graph convolutional network (GCN), a graph attention network (GAT), and a graph-f ree multi-layer perceptron (MLP), using identical features and training procedures for t he learned models, against a Naive Bayes reference. Across 100 runs on ten open-source J ava and C/C++ systems, all three neural models outperform the reference on both F1 metri cs. GAT achieves the highest averages of system-level median macro and micro F1, at 83.5
  and 92.2, respectively, whereas MLP remains competitive or superior on several systems.
  On Chrome, the largest system with 18,343 files, GAT and GCN exceed MLP by 22 and 19 ma cro-F1 points, respectively, and have substantially fewer persistently misclassified fil es. Connectivity and homophily are associated with where neural message passing helps, w hile multi-relational aggregation outperforms a single merged edge type on every system for both GCN and GAT. A follow-up study finds that several model rankings depend on the training seed, qualifying smaller advantages without removing the large graph benefit ob served on Chrome.

</details>


### [75] [Systematic Detection of Energy Regressions in Software Version Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19895)
*Jérôme Maquoi,François Bechet,Luís Cruz,Benoît Vanderose,Xavier Devroey*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures, submitted to Information and Software Technology journal

Selection reason: EnergyTrackr自动检测提交级能耗回退，含线性与二分搜索算法对比，属程序性能/回归分析的可迁移方法。

TL;DR: 提出 EnergyTrackr 用于跨多个提交检测能耗回归；在五个 Java 项目的 1,932 次提交上评估，二分搜索算法与线性算法效果相当，但资源消耗和处理时间更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色软件工程需要应对信息技术能耗增长，但跨提交自动、高效识别能耗回归的方法仍不足，尤其缺少基于统计显著能耗变化的提交级检测。

Method: 提出 EnergyTrackr，并在五个 Java 项目的 1,932 次提交上进行实证评估，检验其识别显著能耗变化的能力；同时比较分析整个提交历史的线性算法与更高效的二分搜索算法。

Result: EnergyTrackr 能有效识别跨提交的显著能耗变化；二分搜索算法可揭示整体软件能耗趋势，结果与线性方法相当，并减少资源消耗和处理时间。

Conclusion: 期望 EnergyTrackr 帮助开发者准确监控项目中的能耗回归与改进，并优化源代码以降低软件能耗。

中文摘要：背景：绿色软件工程正成为应对信息技术日益增长的能源影响的关键举措，尤其是在持续开发中。然而，在设计自动化方法以高效识别跨提交的能耗回归方面仍存在挑战。特别是，鲜有工作致力于通过识别能耗的统计显著变化来自动检测提交级别的回归。目标：本文提出 EnergyTrackr，一种用于检测跨多个提交的能耗回归的方法。方法：我们在来自五个 Java 项目的 1,932 次提交上对 EnergyTrackr 进行了实证评估，评估其识别显著能耗变化的能力。我们还比较了一种分析整个提交历史的简单线性算法与一种更高效的二分搜索算法。结果：评估表明 EnergyTrackr 在识别跨提交的显著能耗变化方面有效。我们的发现凸显了二分搜索算法在揭示整体软件能耗趋势方面的效率，其取得的结果与线性方法相当，同时减少了资源消耗和处理时间。结论：我们期望 EnergyTrackr 能帮助开发者准确监控其项目中的能耗回归和改进，并优化其源代码以降低软件能耗。

Original abstract: Context: Green software engineering is emerging as a crucial response to the rising energy impact of information technology, especially in continuous development. However, challenges remain in devising automated methods to efficiently identify energy regressions across commits. In particular, little effort has been put into automatically detecting regressions at the commit level by identifying statistically significant changes in energy consumption. Objectives: This paper presents EnergyTrackr, an approach for detecting energy regressions across multiple commits. Methods: We empirically evaluated EnergyTrackr on 1,932 commits from five Java projects, assessing its ability to identify significant energy changes. We also compared a straightforward linear algorithm that analyzes the entire commit history with a more efficient dichotomous search algorithm. Results: The evaluation demonstrates the effectiveness of EnergyTrackr in identifying significant energy changes across commits. Our findings highlight the efficiency of the dichotomous algorithm in uncovering overall software energy trends, achieving results comparable to the linear approach while reducing resource consumption and processing time. Conclusion: We expect EnergyTrackr to help developers accurately monitor energy regressions and improvements in their projects and optimize their source code to reduce software energy consumption.

</details>


### [76] [Temporal Adaptive Fine-Tuning for Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19970)
*Alex Wolf,Pooja Rani,Harald C. Gall*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: TAFT以仓库演化为监督做时序自适应微调，处理知识更新/抑制/保留，可迁移到代码agent的记忆与知识维护机制。

TL;DR: 提出TAFT，将仓库变迁作为监督信号，通过偏好优化和保留示例重放，使仓库级代码理解QA模型随时间适应代码演进，并在时间性SWE-bench上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库专用LLM会随代码演进失准；现有检索、微调和模型编辑方法只覆盖部分问题，需要同时获取新知识、抑制过时知识、保留仍有效知识并维持语义邻居稳定。

Method: TAFT从仓库变迁生成具有时间基础的QA数据；通过偏好优化配对新增有效知识与过时知识；重放以变迁为条件的保留示例以保留仍有效知识；在增强的时间性SWE-bench的十个时间切片、255,295条QA上评估，比较延迟式Baseline、按切片重训练式Baseline-Batched及带知识锚点变体，并从更新知识、过时答案抑制、保留、邻居稳定性四个维度衡量。

Result: TAFT持续优于延迟式和批处理式基线：更新/抑制知识准确率平均相对提升18%，保留准确率翻倍，邻居准确率约为1.5倍；直接提供知识锚点时，TAFT-KANT在平均更新/抑制、保留和邻居准确率上分别再提升3.55%、8.35%和13.00%。

Conclusion: TAFT在所有评估维度均有效，能够使代码理解QA与仓库演进保持对齐。

中文摘要：仓库级代码理解问答（QA）支持开发者对分布在整个代码库中的功能与用法进行推理。然而，随着代码演进，面向特定仓库专门化的大语言模型（LLM）可能会变得失准。我们将这一挑战作为时间性仓库适应来研究：获取新知识、抑制过时知识、保留仍然有效的知识，以及保持语义相邻知识。现有的检索、微调和模型编辑方法仅能解决该问题的部分方面。我们提出时间自适应微调（TAFT），这是一种新颖的适应方法，将仓库变迁视为监督信号。TAFT从仓库变迁中导出具有时间基础的QA数据，通过偏好优化将新近有效知识与过时知识配对，并重放以变迁为条件的保留示例，以保留仍然有效的知识。我们在一个增强的时间性SWE-bench数据集上评估TAFT，该数据集包含跨越十个时间切片的255,295条QA条目，沿四个维度比较延迟式（Baseline）、按切片重新训练式（Baseline-Batched）以及带有知识锚点的变体：更新知识、过时答案抑制、保留和邻居稳定性。TAFT持续优于延迟式和批处理式基线，在更新/抑制知识准确率上平均相对提升18%，保留准确率翻倍，邻居准确率约为1.5倍。在直接提供知识锚点的情况下，TAFT-KANT在平均更新/抑制、保留和邻居准确率上分别比TAFT进一步提高3.55%、8.35%和13.00%。总体而言，TAFT在所有评估领域均展示了有效性，有效实现了代码理解QA与仓库演进的对齐。

Original abstract: Repository-level code comprehension question answering (QA) supports developer reasoning over functionality and usage distributed across codebases. However, repository-specialized large language models (LLMs) can become misaligned as code evolves. We study this challenge as temporal repository adaptation: acquiring new knowledge, suppressing obsolete, retaining still-valid, and preserving semantically neighboring knowledge. Existing retrieval, fine-tuning, and model-editing methods address only parts of this problem. We propose Temporal Adaptive Fine-Tuning (TAFT), a novel adaptation approach that treats repository transitions as supervision. TAFT derives temporally grounded QA data from repository transitions, pairs newly valid with obsolete knowledge through preference optimization, and replays transition-conditioned retention examples to preserve still-valid knowledge. We evaluate TAFT on an augmented temporal SWE-bench dataset comprising 255,295 QA entries across ten temporal slices, comparing deferred (Baseline), per-slice retrained (Baseline-Batched), and variants with knowledge anchors along four dimensions: updated knowledge, obsolete-answer suppression, retention, and neighbor stability. TAFT consistently outperforms both the deferred and batched baselines, with an average relative improvement of 18% in updated/suppressed knowledge accuracy, doubled retention accuracy, and roughly 1.5 times the neighbor accuracy. With directly supplied knowledge anchors, TAFT-KANT further improves mean updated/suppressed, retention, and neighbor accuracy over TAFT by 3.55%, 8.35%, and 13.00%, respectively. Overall, TAFT demonstrates its effectiveness across all evaluated areas, effectively enabling code comprehension QA alignment with repository evolution.

</details>


### [77] [LintEval: Benchmarking LLMs on Linter-Guided File Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20027)
*Tongwei Deng,Jinyu Zhu,Yue Cao,Chenxu Wang,Yanzhen Zou,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Code available at https://github.com/6643789wsx/LintEval

Selection reason: LintEval多语言linter引导文件重构基准，联合评估功能保持、告警减少与最小编辑，直接面向代码agent重构能力。

TL;DR: 提出 LintEval 多语言基准，评估 linter 引导的文件重构；实验显示功能保持与告警减少之间存在持续权衡，且面向重构的 SFT 可显著提升开源模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准未明确评估 linter 引导的文件重构，即模型需在保持功能的同时减少源文件中的 lint 告警，因此需要专门基准来联合衡量功能保持与告警减少等目标。

Method: 构建 LintEval 多语言基准，覆盖 C++、Python 和 Java，包含 13,061 个具有正确初始功能的程序和 171,662 条由特定语言 linter 提取的 lint 告警，并联合评估功能保持、lint 告警减少、新告警避免和编辑最小性。

Result: 实验显示出持续权衡：闭源商业模型最高达 90.4% Reduction@P，但 Pass@1 仅为 67.1%-68.3%；若干开源小模型 Reduction@P 较低甚至为负。受控微调研究表明监督格式重要，面向重构的 SFT 将 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 的 Reduction@P 从 24.7% 提升至 76.0%。

Conclusion: LintEval 支持对在功能保持条件下改进源文件的模型进行受控评估。

中文摘要：代码检查工具（Linters）广泛用于代码维护和实时 IDE 编辑中，以在源文件中暴露与性能、风格、易错和其他模式相关的 lint 告警。然而，现有的代码基准并未明确评估由 linter 引导的文件重构，即模型必须在满足功能保持的前提下，减少具有正确初始功能的源文件中的 lint 告警。我们提出了 LintEval，一个面向 C++、Python 和 Java 的、由 linter 引导的文件重构多语言基准，包含 13,061 个具有正确初始功能的程序，以及使用特定语言 linter 提取的 171,662 条 lint 告警。LintEval 联合评估功能保持、lint 告警减少、新告警避免和编辑最小性。实验揭示了一个持续存在的权衡：闭源商业模型达到最高 90.4% 的 Reduction@P，但仅实现 67.1%-68.3% 的 Pass@1，而若干开源小模型表现出较低或负的 Reduction@P。一项受控微调研究进一步表明，监督格式很重要：面向重构的 SFT 将 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 的 Reduction@P 从 24.7% 提高到 76.0%。LintEval 能够对在功能保持下改进源文件的模型进行受控评估。

Original abstract: Linters are widely used in code maintenance and real-time IDE editing to surface lint alerts related to performance, style, error-prone, and other patterns in source files. However, existing code benchmarks do not explicitly evaluate linter-guided file refactoring, where models must reduce lint alerts in source files with correct initial functionality while satisfying functionality preservation. We introduce LintEval, a multilingual benchmark for linter-guided file refactoring across C++, Python, and Java, comprising 13,061 programs with correct initial functionality and 171,662 lint alerts extracted using language-specific linters. LintEval jointly evaluates functionality preservation, lint alert reduction, new alert avoidance, and edit minimality. Experiments reveal a persistent trade-off: closed-source commercial models reach up to 90.4% Reduction@P but achieve only 67.1%-68.3% Pass@1, while several open-source small models show low or negative Reduction@P. A controlled fine-tuning study further shows that supervision format matters: refactoring-oriented SFT raises Qwen2.5-Coder-7B Reduction@P from 24.7% to 76.0%. LintEval enables controlled evaluation of models that improve source files under functionality preservation.

</details>


### [78] [SWE-Nexus: Automated Composition of Software Engineering Tasks from Test Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20038)
*Songwei Yu,Ziqi Miao,Jingxuan Xu,Junhao Fan,Haoyang Huang,Yuan Xiong,Zihang Xu,Weihao Li,Wenqiang Zhu,Yuechen Luo,Xinshun Feng,Yanan Wu,Ken Deng*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: SWE-Nexus用测试执行自动组合仓库级实现任务并构建600任务基准，属agent训练/评测数据构造的可迁移方法。

TL;DR: SWE-Nexus 通过测试执行追踪与掩码构建仓库级实现任务，形成覆盖 105 个仓库、6 种语言的 600 个任务基准，最佳配置一次尝试解决 37.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史代码变更虽提供任务边界，但不能识别测试覆盖的生产代码函数，也不能在单个变更之外组合已验证行为，因此需要从测试执行自动构建仓库级任务。

Method: SWE-Nexus 全自动流水线含四个构建智能体：选择测试、适配追踪与评估协议、生成行为规范；追踪记录生产代码函数体执行足迹，保留满足评分通过且掩码引发失败或错误的作用域，并确定性组合兼容作用域、共享函数去重且重新验证。

Result: 基准含 600 个任务（105 个仓库、6 种语言），其中 116 个原子任务、484 个复合任务；在 3 种智能体脚手架下评估 12 个模型，最佳配置一次尝试解决 37.8%，作用域加权评分衡量部分恢复。

Conclusion: SWE-Nexus 支持基于已验证行为、超越单个历史变更来构建任务，扩展了任务来源。

中文摘要：历史代码变更提供了便捷的任务边界，但无法识别所选测试执行了哪些生产代码函数，也无法支持在单个变更之外组合已验证行为。我们提出 SWE-Nexus，一个全自动流水线，从测试执行构建仓库级实现任务。四个构建智能体选择测试、为每个仓库调整追踪与评估协议，并生成行为规范。对于每个选定的测试文件，追踪记录生产代码函数体的观测执行足迹。仅当所有预期评分点在原始仓库中通过，并且屏蔽被追踪的函数体导致至少一次失败或错误时，才保留一个作用域。兼容的作用域被确定性地组合，共享函数只计一次，并且每个组成测试文件都在完整组合掩码下重新验证。该基准包含来自 105 个仓库、涵盖六种编程语言的 600 个任务，其中包括 116 个原子任务和 484 个复合任务。我们在三种智能体脚手架下评估了 12 个模型。最佳配置在一次尝试中解决了 37.8% 的任务，而作用域加权评分衡量部分恢复。SWE-Nexus 支持基于已验证行为、超越单个历史变更来构建任务。

Original abstract: Historical code changes provide convenient task boundaries, but do not identify which production functions selected tests exercise or support composing validated behaviors beyond a single change. We present SWE-Nexus, a fully automated pipeline that constructs repository-level implementation tasks from test execution. Four construction agents select tests, adapt tracing and evaluation protocols to each repository, and generate behavioral specifications. For each selected test file, tracing records an observed execution footprint of production function bodies. A scope is retained only if all expected scoring points pass in the original repository and masking the traced bodies causes at least one failure or error. Compatible scopes are composed deterministically, with shared functions counted once and every constituent test file revalidated under the complete combined mask. The benchmark contains 600 tasks from 105 repositories across six programming languages, including 116 atomic and 484 composite tasks. We evaluate 12 models under three agent scaffolds. The best configuration resolves 37.8% of tasks in one attempt, and scope-weighted scoring measures partial recovery. SWE-Nexus enables task construction from validated behavior beyond individual historical changes.

</details>


### [79] [The Code of Theseus: Can Agents Rebuild Software from Recovered Design Documents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20068)
*Cezar Sas,Sandra Mitrović,Fabio Barth,Georg Rehm,Matteo Salani*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables

Selection reason: 从代码恢复设计与需求文档并用coding agent重建软件，衡量设计级信息对模块结构的影响，可迁移到agent规划与设计上下文。

TL;DR: 论文提出从紧凑仓库表示、静态依赖图和关键上下文文件中恢复对齐的利益相关者意图、需求与架构，并发布 10,764 个仓库的制品数据集；实验表明这些制品虽不显著提升重建代码的留出集通过率，却显著改善模块结构相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体日益需要先设计后实现，但训练与评测数据集中于实现和测试，需求与设计制品常缺失、过时或私密；源代码虽丰富，现有架构恢复方法却上下文饱和、难扩展且只能产出单一制品。

Method: 提出框架，从紧凑仓库表示、静态依赖图和关键上下文文件恢复对齐的意图、需求与架构；发布 10,764 个 Java、Python、C/C++、C# 仓库制品数据集；用 LLM 评判器评估三个生成器；并通过软件版忒修斯之船实验，让编码智能体仅凭意图或结合恢复的需求与架构，在仅给接口契约和部分测试下重建 29 个 Commit0 库。

Result: LLM 评判器认为三个生成器的制品清晰且内部一致，但正确性与抽象层级可靠性较低。重建实验中，制品未显著改变留出集通过率（33.7% 对 35.8%），但显著提升模块结构相似度（模块 F1 从 0.54 到 0.63），且不同生成器和简洁制品均有效。

Conclusion: 恢复的制品承载了测试未捕捉的设计信息，可作为智能体先设计后实现的可扩展设计级数据来源；该往返实验无需人工参考即可度量这一效果。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体越来越多地在实现系统之前先进行设计，但用于训练和评估它们的数据却集中在实现与测试上：需求和设计制品常常缺失、过时或私密。相比之下，源代码则非常丰富。先前工作通过将源代码喂给 LLM 或让智能体探索源代码来恢复架构文档，但这种方式会使上下文饱和，无法扩展到大量代码仓库，并且只能产出单一制品，而非相互对齐的制品。
我们提出了一个框架，可从紧凑的仓库表示、静态依赖图和关键上下文文件中恢复相互对齐的利益相关者意图、需求和架构，并将 10,764 个 Java、Python、C/C++ 和 C# 仓库的制品作为数据集发布。一个 LLM 评判器将三个生成器产出的制品评为清晰且内部一致，但在正确性和恰当的抽象层级方面可靠性较低。
为了检验这些制品是否承载设计信息，我们提出一个软件版忒修斯之船：一个编码智能体仅根据意图，或结合恢复出的需求与架构，在只给定接口契约和部分测试的情况下重建 29 个 Commit0 库。这些制品并未显著改变留出集通过率（33.7% 对 35.8%），但使重建后的模块结构显著更接近原始结构（模块 F1 从 0.54 提升到 0.63），无论使用哪个生成器，甚至使用简洁制品时也是如此。
因此，恢复出的制品传达了测试未能捕捉的设计，为在实现前先设计的智能体提供了一种可扩展的设计级数据来源，而这一往返过程无需人工参考即可对其进行度量。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly design a system before implementing it, yet the data to train and evaluate them centres on implementation and testing: requirements and design artefacts are often missing, outdated or private. Source code, in contrast, is abundant. Prior work recovers architecture documentation by feeding LLMs the source code or letting agents explore it, which saturates the context, does not scale to many repositories, and yields a single artefact rather than aligned ones.
  We present a framework that recovers aligned stakeholder intent, requirements and architecture from a compact repository representation, a static dependency graph and key contextual files, and release the artefacts of 10,764 Java, Python, C/C++ and C# repositories as a dataset. An LLM judge rates the artefacts of three generators as clear and internally consistent, but less reliably correct and at the right level of abstraction.
  To test whether the artefacts carry the design, we pose a software Ship of Theseus: a coding agent rebuilds 29 Commit0 libraries from the intent alone or with the recovered requirements and architecture, given only an interface contract and part of the tests. The artefacts do not significantly change the held-out pass rate (33.7% vs. 35.8%), but bring the rebuilt module structure significantly closer to the original (module F1 from 0.54 to 0.63), with either generator and even with concise artefacts.
  Recovered artefacts thus convey design that tests do not capture, offering a scalable source of design-level data for agents that design before they implement, and the round trip measures this without human references.

</details>


### [80] [SWE-Stream: Scaling Executable Training Data for SWE Agents via Progressive Construction and Elastic Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20193)
*Chengyang Liu,Wen Yang,Shifeng Chen,Delai Qiu,Ji Yang,Junnan Zhu,Yining Wang,Shengping Liu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: SWE-Stream用渐进构建与弹性流式执行规模化可执行SWE训练数据，直接提升SWE agent训练数据与resolve率。

TL;DR: SWE-Stream 通过渐进式构建与弹性流式执行，从开发历史中规模化生成可执行 SWE 训练数据；基于 10,728 个仓库构建 80,874 个任务、收集 118,688 条成功轨迹，微调 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 后在 SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual、Pro 上分别达到 69.0%、49.3%、45.42% 解决率，并在受控比较中优于三个现有数据源，同时提升构建效率与任务接受量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体发展迅速，但其训练受限于高质量可执行任务不足；开发历史虽包含构建此类任务的信号，却因环境不完整、测试缺失和工件不一致而常需大量重建。

Method: 提出 SWE-Stream：渐进式验证与精炼任务候选，优先复用现有工件，必要时进行定向补全；弹性调度器在共享资源约束下异步协调构建阶段，并依据工作负载调整执行预算。

Result: 使用六种编程语言、10,728 个仓库的开发历史，构建 80,874 个已验证任务并收集 118,688 条成功轨迹；微调 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 后在 SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual、Pro 上解决率分别为 69.0%、49.3%、45.42%。在训练预算大致匹配的受控比较中，SWE-bench Verified 解决率为 45.6%，优于三个现有数据源；流式构建吞吐量为批式构建的 1.45 倍，比固定限制流式多接受 13.2% 任务。

Conclusion: SWE-Stream 能有效扩展可执行 SWE 训练数据并提升智能体性能，同时通过弹性流式构建提高数据构建效率与任务接受量。

中文摘要：软件工程智能体已快速发展，但其训练规模化仍受限于高质量可执行任务的有限可用性。开发历史包含用于构建此类任务的有价值信号，然而由于环境不完整、测试缺失和工件不一致，它们通常需要大量重建。我们提出 SWE-Stream，一个通过渐进式构建和弹性流式执行来规模化可执行 SWE 训练数据的系统。SWE-Stream 渐进式地验证并精炼任务候选，尽可能复用现有工件，并仅在必要时应用针对性补全。一个弹性调度器在共享资源约束下异步协调构建阶段，根据观察到的工作负载需求调整执行预算。使用来自六种编程语言的 10,728 个代码仓库的开发历史，SWE-Stream 构建了 80,874 个已验证任务，并收集了 118,688 条成功轨迹。在这些轨迹上微调 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507，在 SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual 和 Pro 上分别达到 69.0%、49.3% 和 45.42% 的解决率。在训练预算大致匹配的受控比较中，SWE-Stream 在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 45.6% 的解决率，优于三个现有数据源。此外，其流式构建策略提高了效率，提供批处理构建的 1.45 倍已接受任务吞吐量，并且比固定限制流式处理多接受 13.2% 的任务。

Original abstract: Software engineering agents have advanced rapidly, but scaling their training remains constrained by the limited availability of high-quality executable tasks. Development histories contain valuable signals for constructing such tasks, yet they often require substantial reconstruction due to incomplete environments, missing tests, and inconsistent artifacts. We present SWE-Stream, a system for scaling executable SWE training data through progressive construction and elastic streaming execution. SWE-Stream progressively validates and refines task candidates, reusing existing artifacts when possible and applying targeted completion only when necessary. An elastic scheduler asynchronously coordinates construction stages under shared resource constraints, adapting execution budgets based on observed workload demands. Using development histories from 10,728 repositories across six programming languages, SWE-Stream constructs 80,874 validated tasks and collects 118,688 successful trajectories. Fine-tuning Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 on these trajectories achieves resolve rates of 69.0%, 49.3%, and 45.42% on SWE-bench Verified, Multilingual, and Pro, respectively. In a controlled comparison with approximately matched training budgets, SWE-Stream achieves a 45.6% resolve rate on SWE-bench Verified, outperforming three existing data sources. Moreover, its streaming construction strategy improves efficiency, delivering $1.45\times$ the accepted-task throughput of batch construction and accepting 13.2% more tasks than fixed-limit streaming.

</details>


### [81] [Doc2Harness: Benchmarking LLMs on Long-Horizon Harness Engineering of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20344)
*Yuyao Ge,Shenghua Liu,Peizhong Gao,Chendi Ge,Chenxu Liu,Yiwei Wang,Lingrui Mei,Baolong Bi,Jiayu Yao,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables

Selection reason: Doc2Harness首个针对长程harness工程的可确定基准，评测agent脚手架构建能力，属agent工作流/评测机制的可迁移贡献。

TL;DR: Doc2Harness 是一个两阶段、确定性的长时程 harness 工程基准：构建模型在固定构建 harness 下生成候选 harness，随后冻结候选 harness 由脚本化模拟模型端到端测试，并用多个可观测通道评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只在固定 harness 下测下游任务成功率，未评估模型构建或工程化 harness 本身的能力；而智能体在长时程真实工作空间中的表现高度依赖 harness。

Method: 两阶段：Stage 1 构建模型基于仅接口契约骨架和整合十二个前沿 harness 行为的开发文档，在隔离容器中构建完整候选 harness；Stage 2 在独立容器中逐用例运行冻结候选 harness，由脚本化模拟模型端到端驱动，通过模型请求、磁盘文件、退出码和命令输出四通道评分，并设阳性对照。

Result: 在 19 个前沿模型、328 个测试用例、每个模型 3 次独立构建的评估中，GPT-6 Astra 通过率最高为 53.05%，最弱模型为 27.44%，相差约 26 个百分点；最强模型仍失败近半数用例，失败主要集中于横切功能跨模块拆分。

Conclusion: Doc2Harness 据称是首个直接衡量长时程 harness 工程的确定性基准，揭示当前前沿模型在该能力上仍有显著不足。

中文摘要：由大语言模型驱动的智能体越来越多地被部署到长时程任务中，在这些任务里它们使用工具并修改真实工作空间。它们的表现如何，既取决于模型本身，也同样取决于模型周围的执行框架（harness）。现有基准在固定 harness 下衡量下游任务成功率，使 harness 工程本身未被探索。我们提出 Doc2Harness，一个两阶段基准，直接评估长时程 harness 工程。在第一阶段，一个在固定构建者 harness 内运行的构建模型接收一个仅含接口的契约骨架，以及一份整合了十二个前沿 harness 行为的开发文档，然后在一个隔离容器中构建完整的候选 harness。在第二阶段，冻结的候选 harness 在与构建环境分离的容器中运行，每个测试用例一个容器，并由一个脚本化模拟模型端到端驱动。我们使用四个可观测通道对每个测试用例评分，即发送给模型的请求、磁盘上的文件、进程退出码和命令输出；阳性对照排除空洞通过。在十九个前沿模型上，每个模型用三次独立构建在 328 个测试用例上评估，GPT-6 Astra 取得最高通过率 53.05%，而最弱模型达到 27.44%，差距约二十六个百分点。即使最强模型也在近一半测试用例上失败，且失败集中在横切功能如何跨模块拆分。据我们所知，Doc2Harness 提供了首个用于衡量长时程 harness 工程的确定性基准。

Original abstract: Agents driven by large language models are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks, where they use tools and modify real workspaces. How well they perform depends as much on the harness around the model as on the model itself. Existing benchmarks measure downstream task success under a fixed harness, leaving harness engineering itself unexplored. We propose Doc2Harness, a two-stage benchmark that evaluates long-horizon harness engineering directly. In Stage 1, a build model operating inside a fixed builder harness receives an interface-only contract skeleton and a development document that consolidates behavior across twelve frontier harnesses, and then constructs a complete candidate harness in an isolated container. In Stage 2, the frozen candidate harness runs in containers separate from the construction environment, one per test case, and is driven end to end by a scripted mock model. We score each test case using four observable channels, namely the requests sent to the model, the files on disk, the process exit code, and the command output; positive controls rule out vacuous passes. Across nineteen frontier models, each evaluated with three independent builds on 328 test cases, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest pass rate at 53.05%, while the weakest model reaches 27.44%, a gap of roughly twenty-six percentage points. Even the strongest model fails nearly half of the test cases, and failures cluster around how cross-cutting functionality is split across modules. To our knowledge, Doc2Harness provides the first deterministic benchmark for measuring long-horizon harness engineering.

</details>
